<script>
/* ===================== Page logic (no need to edit) ===================== */
const PUBLIC_BUILD = false;   // the "public" download flips this to true: no developer panel, no stored login
const DRAFT_KEY = "ppz_draft", UNLOCK_KEY = "ppz_unlock_until", SESSION_HOURS = 10;
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
const esc = s => String(s ?? "").replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const bold = s => esc(s).replace(/\*\*(.+?)\*\*/g, "<b>$1</b>");
const STATUS = {
  working:     { cls: "working", label: "working!" },
  unknown:     { cls: "unknown", label: "???" },
  unknownlink: { cls: "unknown", label: "Unknown Link?" },
  blocked:     { cls: "blocked", label: "blocked" }
};
const st = s => STATUS[s] || STATUS.unknown;

// Load draft from this device if one exists
const BUILD = "2026-11-07-g";   // bump this whenever the page code/data is replaced with a new version
const normalize = d => { d._build = BUILD; d.canvas = d.canvas || { height: 520, items: [] }; return d; };
const EXPORTED_DATA = normalize(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(DATA)));   // what this file shipped with
let D = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(DATA)), hasDraft = false;
try {
  const saved = PUBLIC_BUILD ? null : localStorage.getItem(DRAFT_KEY);
  if (saved) {
    const d = JSON.parse(saved);
    if (d._build === BUILD) { D = d; hasDraft = true; }
    else localStorage.removeItem(DRAFT_KEY);   // leftover draft from an older version: ignore it
  }
} catch (e) {}
normalize(D);
let undoStack = [];
let lastState = JSON.stringify(D);
let lastUndoAt = 0;

const img = key => (D.images || {})[key] || "";

/* ---------- Public page ---------- */
let pvIdx = -1; // which usable link the preview is showing
let tileAnim = true; // cards slide in only on the first draw, not on every search keystroke
function tile(p, i) {
  const src = img(p.image);
  const pic = src
    ? `<img class="pic ${p.imageFit === "contain" ? "image-fit" : ""}" src="${esc(src)}" alt="${esc(p.name)}" loading="lazy">`
    : `<div class="ph">${esc((p.name || "?").charAt(0))}</div>`;
  const dots = (p.links || []).map(l => `<i class="dot ${st(l.status).cls}"></i>`).join("");
  const enter = tileAnim ? ` enter" style="animation-delay:${Math.min(i, 12) * 70}ms` : "";
  return `<div class="tile${enter}" tabindex="0" data-i="${i}">${pic}
    <div class="info"><h3>${esc(p.name)}</h3><small>click to see more</small>
    <div class="mini">${dots}</div></div></div>`;
}
function renderTop() {
  const list = D.proxies.map((p, i) => [p, i]).filter(([p]) => p.top).slice(0, 5);
  $("top5").innerHTML = list.map(([p, i]) => tile(p, i)).join("") || `<div class="empty">No top picks yet.</div>`;
}
function renderAll() {
  const q = $("search").value.toLowerCase().trim();
  const list = D.proxies.map((p, i) => [p, i]).filter(([p]) =>
    [p.name, p.aka, p.note, ...(p.tags || [])].join(" ").toLowerCase().includes(q));
  $("all").innerHTML = list.map(([p, i]) => tile(p, i)).join("") || `<div class="empty">No proxies match.</div>`;
}
function renderPage() {
  const S = D.site;
  $("siteTitle").textContent = S.title;
  $("siteTagline").textContent = S.tagline;
  $("siteVersion").textContent = S.version;
  $("scrollHint").textContent = S.scrollHint;
  $("topText").textContent = S.topText;
  $("tips").textContent = S.tips;
  $("footer").textContent = S.footer;
  document.title = S.title;
  $("updatesBox").hidden = !D.updates.length;
  $("updates").innerHTML = D.updates.map(u => `<li>${bold(u)}</li>`).join("");
  $("rules").innerHTML = D.rules.map(r => `<li>${bold(r)}</li>`).join("");
  $("soonBox").hidden = !D.comingSoon.items.length;
  $("soonText").textContent = D.comingSoon.text;
  $("soonItems").innerHTML = D.comingSoon.items.map(i => `<span class="tag">${esc(i)}</span>`).join("");
  renderTop(); renderAll();
}
let renderTimer;
const queueRender = () => { clearTimeout(renderTimer); renderTimer = setTimeout(renderPage, 250); };

function openProxy(i) {
  const p = D.proxies[i]; if (!p) return;
  pvIdx = -1;
  const src = img(p.image);
  $("sheet").innerHTML = `
    <button class="close" aria-label="Close">×</button>
    ${src ? `<img class="${p.imageFit === "contain" ? "image-fit" : ""}" src="${esc(src)}" alt="${esc(p.name)}">` : ""}
    <h2>${esc(p.name)}</h2>
    ${p.aka ? `<div class="aka">${esc(p.aka)}</div>` : ""}
    <div class="note">${esc(p.note)}</div>
    <div class="tags">${(p.tags || []).map(t => `<span class="tag">${esc(t)}</span>`).join("")}</div>
    ${p.links?.length ? `
      <button class="btn preview-btn" data-act="previewSite" data-i="${i}">◀ Preview site</button>
      <div class="preview-box" id="previewBox"></div>
    ` : ""}
    <div class="links">${(p.links || []).map((l, n) => {
      const s = st(l.status);
      return `<a class="link ${s.cls}" href="${esc(l.url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">
        <i class="dot"></i>Link ${n + 1}<span class="st">${s.label}</span></a>`;
    }).join("")}</div>`;
  $("modal").classList.add("open");
}
const closeModal = () => $("modal").classList.remove("open");

/* ---------- Developer panel ---------- */
const hash = (str, seed = 7) => {
  let h1 = 0xdeadbeef ^ seed, h2 = 0x41c6ce57 ^ seed;
  for (let i = 0, ch; i < str.length; i++) {
    ch = str.charCodeAt(i);
    h1 = Math.imul(h1 ^ ch, 2654435761);
    h2 = Math.imul(h2 ^ ch, 1597334677);
  }
  h1 = Math.imul(h1 ^ (h1 >>> 16), 2246822507) ^ Math.imul(h2 ^ (h2 >>> 13), 3266489909);
  h2 = Math.imul(h2 ^ (h2 >>> 16), 2246822507) ^ Math.imul(h1 ^ (h1 >>> 13), 3266489909);
  return (4294967296 * (2097151 & h2) + (h1 >>> 0)).toString(36);
};
async function hashStr(s) {
  const salted = "ppz|" + s;
  if (window.crypto && crypto.subtle) {
    const buf = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", new TextEncoder().encode(salted));
    return "s:" + [...new Uint8Array(buf)].map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
  }
  return "h:" + hash(salted);
}
const getPath = (o, p) => p.split(".").reduce((a, k) => a?.[k], o);
const setPath = (o, p, v) => {
  const k = p.split("."); let t = o;
  for (let i = 0; i < k.length - 1; i++) t = t[k[i]];
  t[k[k.length - 1]] = v;
};
const isUnlocked = () => +localStorage.getItem(UNLOCK_KEY) > Date.now();
const unlock = () => localStorage.setItem(UNLOCK_KEY, Date.now() + SESSION_HOURS * 3600000);

let tab = "proxies";
const openSet = new Set();

function toast(msg) {
  const t = $("toast"); t.textContent = msg; t.classList.add("show");
  clearTimeout(t._h); t._h = setTimeout(() => t.classList.remove("show"), 2200);
}
function save() {
  const current = JSON.stringify(D);
  if (current !== lastState) {
    // Group rapid typing into one undo step.
    if (Date.now() - lastUndoAt > 700) {
      undoStack.push(lastState);
      if (undoStack.length > 50) undoStack.shift();
      lastUndoAt = Date.now();
    }
    lastState = current;
  }
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(DRAFT_KEY, current);
    hasDraft = true;
  } catch (e) {
    toast("⚠ Could not save on this device. Download your file.");
  }
  queueRender();
}
function updateFab() {
  if (PUBLIC_BUILD) return;
  const fab = $("fab");
  if (!isUnlocked()) { fab.hidden = true; return; }
  const ms = +localStorage.getItem(UNLOCK_KEY) - Date.now();
  const h = Math.floor(ms / 3600000), m = Math.floor((ms % 3600000) / 60000);
  fab.textContent = `⚙ DEV · ${h}h ${m}m`;
  fab.hidden = $("dev").classList.contains("open");
}
setInterval(updateFab, 30000);

function tryOpenDev() {
  if (PUBLIC_BUILD) return;
  if (isUnlocked()) { openDev(); return; }
  const first = !D.devHash;
  $("sheet").innerHTML = `
    <button class="close" aria-label="Close">×</button>
    <h2>${first ? "🔧 Set up login" : "🔒 Developer login"}</h2>
    <p class="sub">${first ? "Choose your username and password. You only do this once." : "Enter your username and password."}</p>
    <input id="userIn" placeholder="Username" autocapitalize="off" autocorrect="off" autocomplete="username" style="margin-bottom:10px">
    <input type="password" id="passIn" placeholder="Password" autocomplete="current-password" style="margin-bottom:8px">
    <label class="check"><input type="checkbox" onchange="$('passIn').type = this.checked ? 'text' : 'password'"> Show password</label>
    <button class="btn pri full" data-act="checkPass">${first ? "Save &amp; unlock" : "Unlock"}</button>`;
  $("modal").classList.add("open");
  setTimeout(() => $("userIn")?.focus(), 50);
}
function openDev() {
  closeModal();
  $("dev").classList.add("open");
  document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
  renderDev(); updateFab();
}
function closeDev() {
  $("dev").classList.remove("open");
  document.body.style.overflow = "";
  if (location.hash === "#dev") history.replaceState(null, "", location.pathname + location.search);
  updateFab();
}

function renderDev() {
  [...$("devTabs").children].forEach(b => b.classList.toggle("on", b.dataset.tab === tab));
  $("devBody").innerHTML = TABS[tab]();
}

function buildHTML(pub = false) {
  const c = document.documentElement.cloneNode(true);
  {  // remove leftovers from the Claude preview or browser extensions, but NEVER the main app, the data or an add-on
    const EXT = ["chrome-" + "extension://", "moz-" + "extension://"];
    const PRE = ["__cowork" + "_html_review_ready__", "__cowork" + "HtmlReviewGuest", "real" + "Parent", "claude-" + "scrollbar", "__cwhr" + "-sel"];
    const hit = (t, list) => list.some(m => t.includes(m));
    c.querySelectorAll("style").forEach(x => { const t = x.textContent; if (hit(t, EXT) || hit(t, PRE)) x.remove(); });
    c.querySelectorAll("script").forEach(x => {
      const t = x.textContent;
      if (x.id || t.includes("const PUBLIC" + "_BUILD")) return;
      if (hit(t, EXT) || hit(t, PRE)) x.remove();
    });
  }
  c.querySelectorAll("[data-dyn]").forEach(e => { e.innerHTML = ""; e.className = e.className.replace(/\bshow\b/, "").trim(); });
  c.querySelector("#modal").classList.remove("open");
  c.querySelector("#dev").classList.remove("open");
  c.querySelector("#fab").hidden = true;
  c.querySelector("body").classList.remove("editing");
  c.querySelector("#edit").hidden = true;
  c.querySelector("body").removeAttribute("style");
  const out = pub ? { ...D, devHash: "" } : D;          // public copy never carries the login
  const json = JSON.stringify(out, null, 2).replace(/<\/script/gi, "<\\/script");
  c.querySelector("#settings").textContent = `\nconst DATA = ${json};\n`;
  if (pub) {
    c.querySelector("#dev").replaceChildren();           // developer panel removed
    c.querySelector("#fab").remove();
    c.querySelectorAll("script").forEach(sc => {
      if (sc.id !== "settings") sc.textContent = sc.textContent.replace(/^const PUBLIC_BUILD = false;/m, "const PUBLIC_BUILD = true;");
    });
  }
  return "<!DOCTYPE html>\n" + c.outerHTML;
}

const ACT = {
  openDev: tryOpenDev,
  downloadPublic() {
    const blob = new Blob([buildHTML(true)], { type: "text/html" });
    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    a.download = "ProxyPathz-public.html";
    document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove();
    setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href), 5000);
    toast("Downloading public version…");
  },
  gAdd(el) {
    if (G.length >= GMAX) return toast("Limit reached: " + GMAX + " items");
    const t = el.dataset.t, tx = t === "text";
    const it = cleanItem({ type: t, x: 30, y: 30, w: tx ? 40 : 25, h: tx ? 12 : 18, text: tx ? "Hello" : "", fill: gNew, color: "#ffffff", size: 18 });
    G.push(it); gSel = it.id; gSave(); renderGuest();
  },
  gDel() { G = G.filter(i => i.id !== gSel); gSel = null; gSave(); renderGuest(); },
  gClear() { if (!confirm("Clear your whole guest canvas?")) return; G = []; gSel = null; gSave(); renderGuest(); },
  gCopy() {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(JSON.stringify({ ppz: 1, items: G.map(({ id, ...r }) => r) }))
      .then(() => toast("Copied! Send this code to the owner.")).catch(() => toast("Copy failed"));
  },
  importGuest() {
    let data; try { data = JSON.parse($("gImport").value); } catch { return toast("That isn't a valid guest code"); }
    const items = (Array.isArray(data?.items) ? data.items : []).slice(0, GMAX).map(cleanItem).filter(Boolean);
    if (!items.length) return toast("No usable items in that code");
    D.canvas.items.push(...items); save(); renderCanvas(); renderDev();
    toast("Added " + items.length + " item(s) to your canvas");
  },
  undo() {
    if (!undoStack.length) return toast("Nothing to undo yet");
    D = JSON.parse(undoStack.pop());
    lastState = JSON.stringify(D);
    try { localStorage.setItem(DRAFT_KEY, lastState); hasDraft = true; } catch (e) {}
    if (!sel()) selId = null;
    renderPage(); renderDev(); renderEd();
    toast("Undid the last change");
  },
  discard() {
    if (!confirm("Discard device-saved edits and restore this file's original settings?")) return;
    D = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(EXPORTED_DATA));
    undoStack = [];
    lastState = JSON.stringify(D);
    localStorage.removeItem(DRAFT_KEY);
    hasDraft = false;
    selId = null;
    renderPage(); renderDev(); renderEd();
    toast("Device edits discarded");
  },
  editCanvas() { closeDev(); document.body.classList.add("editing"); renderCanvas(); renderEd(); $("canvasBox").scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth" }); },
  edDone() { document.body.classList.remove("editing"); selId = null; renderCanvas(); renderEd(); openDev(); },
  edAdd(el) {
    const t = el.dataset.t, tx = t === "text";
    const it = { id: "e" + Date.now().toString(36), type: t, x: 30, y: 30, w: tx ? 40 : 25, h: tx ? 12 : 18,
      text: tx ? "New text" : t === "button" ? "Button" : "", size: 18, color: "#ffffff", fill: newColor,
      fillA: tx ? 0 : 100, op: 0, radius: t === "button" ? 10 : 0, image: "", font: "Default", link: "" };
    D.canvas.items.push(it); selId = it.id; save(); renderCanvas(); renderEd();
  },
  edFwd() { edMove(1); },
  edBack() { edMove(-1); },
  edDel() {
    if (!confirm("Delete this item?")) return;
    D.canvas.items = D.canvas.items.filter(i => i.id !== selId); selId = null; save(); renderCanvas(); renderEd();
  },
  toggleFit(el) {
    const p = D.proxies[+el.dataset.i];
    p.imageFit = p.imageFit === "contain" ? "cover" : "contain";
    save();
    renderDev();
  },
  previewSite(el) {
    const p = D.proxies[+el.dataset.i];
    const box = $("previewBox");
    if (!p || !box) return;
    // Only real web links that aren't marked blocked
    const usable = [];
    (p.links || []).forEach((l, n) => {
      if (l.status === "blocked") return;
      try {
        const u = new URL(l.url);
        if (["https:", "http:"].includes(u.protocol)) usable.push({ n, href: u.href });
      } catch {}
    });
    if (!usable.length) return toast("No usable links to preview");

    pvIdx = (pvIdx + 1) % usable.length;   // every tap moves to the next link
    const cur = usable[pvIdx];

    const cap = document.createElement("div");
    cap.className = "preview-cap";
    cap.textContent = `Showing link ${cur.n + 1} (${pvIdx + 1} of ${usable.length})` +
      (usable.length > 1 ? " · blank? tap again for the next one" : "");

    const frame = document.createElement("iframe");
    frame.src = cur.href;
    frame.title = `Preview of ${p.name}, link ${cur.n + 1}`;
    frame.referrerPolicy = "no-referrer";
    frame.setAttribute("sandbox", "allow-scripts allow-forms");

    const hide = document.createElement("button");
    hide.className = "btn sm";
    hide.dataset.act = "hidePreview";
    hide.textContent = "Hide preview";

    box.replaceChildren(cap, frame, hide);
    box.classList.add("open");
    el.textContent = usable.length > 1 ? "↻ Try next link" : "↻ Reload";
  },
  hidePreview() {
    const box = $("previewBox");
    if (!box) return;
    box.classList.remove("open");
    box.replaceChildren();
    pvIdx = -1;
    const b = document.querySelector(".preview-btn");
    if (b) b.textContent = "◀ Preview site";
  },
  closeDev,
  tab: el => { tab = el.dataset.tab; renderDev(); $("devBody").scrollTop = 0; },
  async checkPass() {
    const u = $("userIn").value.trim(), p = $("passIn").value;
    if (!u || !p) return toast("Enter both username and password");
    const h = await hashStr(u + "\n" + p);
    if (!D.devHash) {
      D.devHash = h; save(); unlock(); openDev();
      toast("Login saved. Download the file to keep it.");
    } else if (h === D.devHash) {
      unlock(); openDev();
    } else {
      toast("Wrong username or password");
    }
  },
  addProxy() {
    D.proxies.push({ name: "New proxy", aka: "", image: "", top: false, note: "", tags: [],
      links: [{ url: "https://", status: "unknown" }] });
    openSet.add(D.proxies.length - 1); save(); renderDev();
  },
  delProxy(el) {
    const i = +el.dataset.i;
    if (!confirm(`Delete "${D.proxies[i].name}"?`)) return;
    D.proxies.splice(i, 1); openSet.clear(); save(); renderDev();
  },
  moveProxy(el) {
    const i = +el.dataset.i, j = i + +el.dataset.d;
    if (j < 0 || j >= D.proxies.length) return;
    [D.proxies[i], D.proxies[j]] = [D.proxies[j], D.proxies[i]];
    openSet.clear(); openSet.add(j); save(); renderDev();
  },
  addLink(el) { D.proxies[+el.dataset.i].links.push({ url: "https://", status: "unknown" }); save(); renderDev(); },
  delLink(el) { D.proxies[+el.dataset.i].links.splice(+el.dataset.j, 1); save(); renderDev(); },
  addStr(el) { getPath(D, el.dataset.list).push(""); save(); renderDev(); },
  delStr(el) { getPath(D, el.dataset.list).splice(+el.dataset.j, 1); save(); renderDev(); },
  addImage() {
    const name = (prompt("Name for this picture (letters/numbers, no spaces):") || "").trim().replace(/[^\w-]/g, "");
    if (!name) return;
    if (name in D.images) return toast("That name already exists");
    D.images[name] = ""; save(); renderDev();
  },
  linkImage(el) {
    const url = prompt("Paste the picture's web link:", D.images[el.dataset.key].startsWith("data:") ? "" : D.images[el.dataset.key]);
    if (url === null) return;
    D.images[el.dataset.key] = url.trim(); save(); renderDev();
  },
  delImage(el) {
    const k = el.dataset.key;
    if (!confirm(`Delete picture "${k}"?`)) return;
    delete D.images[k];
    D.proxies.forEach(p => { if (p.image === k) p.image = ""; });
    save(); renderDev();
  },
  download() {
    const blob = new Blob([buildHTML()], { type: "text/html" });
    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    a.download = "ProxyPathz.html";
    document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove();
    setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href), 5000);
    toast("Downloading…");
  },
  copyHTML() {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(buildHTML())
      .then(() => toast("Copied! Paste it into your file."))
      .catch(() => toast("Copy failed, use Download instead"));
  },
  async setPass() {
    const u = $("newUser").value.trim(), p = $("newPass").value;
    if (!u || !p) return toast("Enter a username and a password");
    D.devHash = await hashStr(u + "\n" + p);
    save(); renderDev();
    toast("Login saved. Download the file to keep it.");
  },
  lock() { localStorage.removeItem(UNLOCK_KEY); closeDev(); toast("Locked"); },
  reset() {
    if (!confirm("Throw away all changes saved on this device?")) return;
    localStorage.removeItem(DRAFT_KEY); location.reload();
  }
};

/* ---------- Events ---------- */
document.addEventListener("click", e => {
  const a = e.target.closest("[data-act]");
  if (a) { e.preventDefault(); ACT[a.dataset.act]?.(a); return; }
  const t = e.target.closest(".tile");
  if (t) return openProxy(+t.dataset.i);
  if (e.target.id === "modal" || e.target.classList.contains("close")) closeModal();
});

function onEdit(e) {
  const el = e.target;
  if (!el.dataset.path || !el.closest("#dev")) return;
  let v = el.type === "checkbox" ? el.checked : el.value;
  if (el.dataset.type === "csv") v = v.split(",").map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  setPath(D, el.dataset.path, v);
  save();
}
document.addEventListener("input", onEdit);
document.addEventListener("change", e => {
  onEdit(e);
  const el = e.target;
  if (el.type === "file" && el.dataset.key && el.files[0]) {
    const f = el.files[0];
    if (f.size > 400 * 1024 && !confirm(`This picture is ${Math.round(f.size / 1024)} KB, which will make your file bigger. Use it anyway?`)) return;
    const r = new FileReader();
    r.onload = () => { D.images[el.dataset.key] = r.result; save(); renderDev(); toast("Picture added"); };
    r.readAsDataURL(f);
  }
});
document.addEventListener("toggle", e => {
  if (!e.target.matches?.("details.card")) return;
  const i = +e.target.dataset.i;
  e.target.open ? openSet.add(i) : openSet.delete(i);
}, true);

let keyBuf = "";
document.addEventListener("keydown", e => {
  if (e.key === "Escape") { closeModal(); if ($("dev").classList.contains("open")) closeDev(); }
  if (e.key === "Enter" && e.target.id === "passIn") ACT.checkPass();
  if (e.key === "Enter" && e.target.classList.contains("tile")) openProxy(+e.target.dataset.i);
  if (e.key.length === 1 && !e.target.matches("input,textarea")) {
    keyBuf = (keyBuf + e.key.toLowerCase()).slice(-7);
    if (keyBuf === "devmode") tryOpenDev();
  }
});

// Secret: tap the title 5 times fast
let taps = 0, tapTimer;
$("siteTitle").addEventListener("click", () => {
  taps++; clearTimeout(tapTimer);
  tapTimer = setTimeout(() => taps = 0, 1500);
  if (taps >= 5) { taps = 0; tryOpenDev(); }
});
// Secret: add #dev to the URL
const checkHash = () => { if (location.hash === "#dev") tryOpenDev(); };
window.addEventListener("hashchange", checkHash);

$("search").addEventListener("input", renderAll);

/* ---------- Canvas ---------- */
let selId = null, snap = true, newColor = "#a66bff";
const FONTS = [["Default","inherit"],["Poppins","'Poppins',sans-serif"],["Orbitron","'Orbitron',sans-serif"],
  ["Russo One","'Russo One',sans-serif"],["Space Grotesk","'Space Grotesk',sans-serif"],["JetBrains Mono","'JetBrains Mono',monospace"]];
const hex = (h, a) => { h = /^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(h) ? h : "#a66bff";
  return `rgba(${parseInt(h.slice(1,3),16)},${parseInt(h.slice(3,5),16)},${parseInt(h.slice(5,7),16)},${a})`; };
const editing = () => document.body.classList.contains("editing");
const safeUrl = u => { try { const x = new URL(u); return ["http:","https:"].includes(x.protocol) ? x.href : ""; } catch { return ""; } };
const sel = () => D.canvas.items.find(i => i.id === selId);

function renderCanvas() {
  const C = D.canvas, cv = $("cv");
  $("canvasBox").hidden = !(editing() || C.items.length);
  cv.style.height = (parseInt(C.height) || 520) + "px";
  cv.replaceChildren();
  C.items.forEach(it => {
    const el = document.createElement("div"), s = el.style;
    el.className = "ci" + (it.id === selId && editing() ? " sel" : "");
    el.dataset.id = it.id;
    s.left = it.x + "%"; s.top = it.y + "%"; s.width = it.w + "%"; s.height = it.h + "%";
    s.borderRadius = it.type === "circle" ? "50%" : (+it.radius || 0) + "px";
    s.opacity = (100 - (+it.op || 0)) / 100;
    s.background = hex(it.fill, (it.fillA ?? 100) / 100);
    s.color = /^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(it.color) ? it.color : "#ffffff";
    s.fontSize = (+it.size || 18) + "px";
    s.fontFamily = (FONTS.find(f => f[0] === it.font) || FONTS[0])[1];
    const src = img(it.image); if (src) s.backgroundImage = `url("${src.replace(/"/g, "%22")}")`;
    if (it.link) s.cursor = "pointer";
    el.textContent = it.text || "";
    const rh = document.createElement("i"); rh.className = "rh"; el.appendChild(rh);
    cv.appendChild(el);
  });
}

function renderEd() {
  const p = $("edit"); p.hidden = !editing(); if (p.hidden) return;
  const it = sel();
  const rng = (k, label) => `<label class="field"><span>${label}</span><input type="range" min="0" max="100" data-k="${k}" value="${it[k] ?? 0}"></label>`;
  const num = (k, label) => `<label class="field"><span>${label}</span><input type="number" data-k="${k}" value="${it[k] ?? 0}"></label>`;
  const col = (k, label) => `<label class="field"><span>${label}</span><input type="color" data-k="${k}" value="${esc(it[k])}"></label>`;
  p.innerHTML = `
    <div class="row">
      <button class="btn sm" data-act="edAdd" data-t="rect">+ Rectangle</button>
      <button class="btn sm" data-act="edAdd" data-t="circle">+ Circle</button>
      <button class="btn sm" data-act="edAdd" data-t="text">+ Text</button>
      <button class="btn sm" data-act="edAdd" data-t="button">+ Button</button>
    </div>
    <div class="row">
      <input type="color" id="newColor" value="${newColor}" style="width:56px;flex:0 0 56px;height:44px;padding:2px">
      <label class="check" style="margin:0;flex:1"><input type="checkbox" id="snapT" ${snap ? "checked" : ""}> Grid snap</label>
      <button class="btn sm" data-act="undo">↶ Undo</button>
      <button class="btn sm pri" data-act="edDone">Done</button>
    </div>` + (!it ? `<p class="sub">Tap an item to edit it. Drag to move it, drag the corner square to resize.</p>` : `
    <label class="field"><span>Text</span><textarea data-k="text">${esc(it.text)}</textarea></label>
    ${num("size", "Font size")}${col("color", "Text color")}
    <label class="field"><span>Font</span><select data-k="font">${FONTS.map(f => `<option ${f[0] === it.font ? "selected" : ""}>${f[0]}</option>`).join("")}</select></label>
    ${col("fill", "Fill color")}${rng("fillA", "Background opacity")}${rng("op", "Transparency")}${num("radius", "Corner radius (px)")}
    <label class="field"><span>Picture</span><select data-k="image"><option value="">(none)</option>${Object.keys(D.images).map(k => `<option value="${esc(k)}" ${k === it.image ? "selected" : ""}>${esc(k)}</option>`).join("")}</select></label>
    <label class="field"><span>Link (opens when tapped on the live page)</span><input data-k="link" value="${esc(it.link)}" placeholder="https://..." inputmode="url"></label>
    <div class="row"><button class="btn sm" data-act="edFwd">↑ Forward</button><button class="btn sm" data-act="edBack">↓ Backward</button>
      <button class="btn sm danger" data-act="edDel" style="margin-left:auto">Delete</button></div>`);
}
function edMove(d) {
  const a = D.canvas.items, i = a.findIndex(x => x.id === selId), j = i + d;
  if (i < 0 || j < 0 || j >= a.length) return;
  [a[i], a[j]] = [a[j], a[i]]; save(); renderCanvas();
}
$("edit").addEventListener("input", e => {
  const el = e.target;
  if (el.id === "newColor") { newColor = el.value; return; }
  if (el.id === "snapT") { snap = el.checked; return; }
  const it = sel(); if (!it || !el.dataset.k) return;
  it[el.dataset.k] = (el.type === "range" || el.type === "number") ? +el.value : el.value;
  save();
});

let drag = null;
$("cv").addEventListener("pointerdown", e => { if (drag) return;
  if (!editing()) return;
  const el = e.target.closest(".ci"), it = el && D.canvas.items.find(i => i.id === el.dataset.id);
  if (!it) return;
  e.preventDefault();
  const changed = selId !== it.id; selId = it.id;
  $("cv").querySelectorAll(".ci.sel").forEach(x => x.classList.remove("sel")); el.classList.add("sel");
  drag = { id: e.pointerId, moved: false, it, el, rs: e.target.classList.contains("rh"), x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY,
    ox: it.x, oy: it.y, ow: it.w, oh: it.h, r: $("cv").getBoundingClientRect() };
  el.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
  if (changed) renderEd();
});
document.addEventListener("pointermove", e => {
  if (!drag || e.pointerId !== drag.id) return; drag.moved = true;
  const dx = (e.clientX - drag.x) / drag.r.width * 100, dy = (e.clientY - drag.y) / drag.r.height * 100;
  const q = v => snap ? Math.round(v / 5) * 5 : Math.round(v * 10) / 10, { it, el } = drag;
  if (drag.rs) { it.w = Math.max(5, Math.min(100 - it.x, q(drag.ow + dx))); it.h = Math.max(5, Math.min(100 - it.y, q(drag.oh + dy))); }
  else { it.x = Math.max(0, Math.min(100 - it.w, q(drag.ox + dx))); it.y = Math.max(0, Math.min(100 - it.h, q(drag.oy + dy))); }
  el.style.left = it.x + "%"; el.style.top = it.y + "%"; el.style.width = it.w + "%"; el.style.height = it.h + "%";
});
const endDrag = e => { if (!drag || e.pointerId !== drag.id) return; const m = drag.moved; drag = null; if (m) save(); };
document.addEventListener("pointerup", endDrag);
document.addEventListener("pointercancel", endDrag);
$("cv").addEventListener("click", e => {
  if (editing()) return;
  const el = e.target.closest(".ci"), it = el && D.canvas.items.find(i => i.id === el.dataset.id), u = it && safeUrl(it.link);
  if (u) window.open(u, "_blank", "noopener");
});

/* ---------- Guest canvas: shapes + text only, locked inside a 540px box, saved per device ---------- */
const GKEY = "ppz_guest", GMAX = 30;
const clamp = (v, a, b) => Math.max(a, Math.min(b, +v || 0));
const hexOk = (h, d) => /^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(h) ? h : d;
function cleanItem(it) {   // used for guest drawings and for codes the owner imports
  if (!it || !["rect", "circle", "text"].includes(it.type)) return null;
  const w = clamp(it.w, 5, 100), h = clamp(it.h, 5, 100);
  return { id: "g" + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9), type: it.type,
    x: clamp(it.x, 0, 100 - w), y: clamp(it.y, 0, 100 - h), w, h,
    text: String(it.text || "").slice(0, 60), size: clamp(it.size || 18, 10, 48),
    color: hexOk(it.color, "#ffffff"), fill: hexOk(it.fill, "#a66bff"),
    fillA: it.type === "text" ? 0 : 100, op: 0, radius: 0, image: "", font: "Default", link: "" };
}
let G = [], gSel = null, gNew = "#a66bff", gDrag = null;
try { G = (JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(GKEY)) || []).slice(0, GMAX).map(cleanItem).filter(Boolean); } catch (e) {}
const gSave = () => { try { localStorage.setItem(GKEY, JSON.stringify(G)); } catch (e) {} };

function paintGuest() {
  const gv = $("gv"); gv.replaceChildren();
  G.forEach(it => {
    const el = document.createElement("div"), st2 = el.style;
    el.className = "ci" + (it.id === gSel ? " sel" : ""); el.dataset.id = it.id;
    st2.left = it.x + "%"; st2.top = it.y + "%"; st2.width = it.w + "%"; st2.height = it.h + "%";
    st2.borderRadius = it.type === "circle" ? "50%" : "0";
    st2.background = hex(it.fill, it.fillA / 100); st2.color = it.color; st2.fontSize = it.size + "px";
    el.textContent = it.text;
    const rh = document.createElement("i"); rh.className = "rh"; el.appendChild(rh); gv.appendChild(el);
  });
}
function toolsGuest() {
  const it = G.find(i => i.id === gSel);
  $("gtools").innerHTML = `<div class="row" style="margin-top:12px;flex-wrap:wrap">
      <button class="btn sm" data-act="gAdd" data-t="rect">+ Rectangle</button>
      <button class="btn sm" data-act="gAdd" data-t="circle">+ Circle</button>
      <button class="btn sm" data-act="gAdd" data-t="text">+ Text</button>
      <input type="color" id="gNew" value="${gNew}" style="width:56px;flex:0 0 56px;height:44px;padding:2px"></div>` +
    (it ? `<label class="field"><span>Text (max 60 letters)</span><input data-g="text" maxlength="60" value="${esc(it.text)}"></label>
      <label class="field"><span>Font size</span><input type="range" min="10" max="48" data-g="size" value="${it.size}"></label>
      <div class="row"><label class="field" style="flex:1"><span>Fill</span><input type="color" data-g="fill" value="${esc(it.fill)}"></label>
        <label class="field" style="flex:1"><span>Text color</span><input type="color" data-g="color" value="${esc(it.color)}"></label></div>
      <button class="btn sm danger" data-act="gDel">Delete item</button>`
    : `<p class="sub">Tap an item to edit it. Drag to move it, drag the corner square to resize.</p>`) +
    `<div class="row" style="margin-top:8px"><button class="btn sm" data-act="gCopy">📋 Copy my canvas</button>
      <button class="btn sm danger" data-act="gClear" style="margin-left:auto">Clear all</button></div>`;
}
function renderGuest() { paintGuest(); toolsGuest(); }
$("gtools").addEventListener("input", e => {
  const el = e.target;
  if (el.id === "gNew") { gNew = hexOk(el.value, gNew); return; }
  const it = G.find(i => i.id === gSel), k = el.dataset.g; if (!it || !k) return;
  it[k] = k === "text" ? el.value.slice(0, 60) : k === "size" ? clamp(el.value, 10, 48) : hexOk(el.value, it[k]);
  gSave(); paintGuest();
});
$("gv").addEventListener("pointerdown", e => { if (gDrag) return;
  const el = e.target.closest(".ci"), it = el && G.find(i => i.id === el.dataset.id); if (!it) return;
  e.preventDefault();
  const changed = gSel !== it.id; gSel = it.id;
  $("gv").querySelectorAll(".ci.sel").forEach(x => x.classList.remove("sel")); el.classList.add("sel");
  gDrag = { id: e.pointerId, it, el, rs: e.target.classList.contains("rh"), x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY,
    ox: it.x, oy: it.y, ow: it.w, oh: it.h, r: $("gv").getBoundingClientRect() };
  el.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
  if (changed) toolsGuest();
});
document.addEventListener("pointermove", e => {
  if (!gDrag || e.pointerId !== gDrag.id) return;
  const d = gDrag, it = d.it, dx = (e.clientX - d.x) / d.r.width * 100, dy = (e.clientY - d.y) / d.r.height * 100;
  if (d.rs) { it.w = clamp(d.ow + dx, 5, 100 - it.x); it.h = clamp(d.oh + dy, 5, 100 - it.y); }
  else { it.x = clamp(d.ox + dx, 0, 100 - it.w); it.y = clamp(d.oy + dy, 0, 100 - it.h); }
  const q = d.el.style; q.left = it.x + "%"; q.top = it.y + "%"; q.width = it.w + "%"; q.height = it.h + "%";
});
const gEnd = e => { if (gDrag && e.pointerId === gDrag.id) { gDrag = null; gSave(); } };
document.addEventListener("pointerup", gEnd);
document.addEventListener("pointercancel", gEnd);
renderGuest();

// Applies the Look settings whenever the page re-renders
function applyLook() {
  document.documentElement.style.setProperty("--accent", D.site.accent || "#a66bff");
  document.body.classList.toggle("noanim", D.site.anim === false);
}
const basePage = renderPage;
renderPage = function () { basePage(); applyLook(); renderCanvas(); $("guestBox").hidden = D.site.guest === false; tileAnim = false; };

renderPage();
updateFab();
checkHash();
</script>


<script id="ppz-addon-visitor">
/* ===== ProxyPathz add-on: visitor settings, favorites, backup ===== */
(() => {
  if (window.__ppzVisitor) return; window.__ppzVisitor = true;
  const VKEY = "proxypathz_v15_visitor";
  let V = { anim: true, guest: true, big: false, text: 100, speed: null, favs: [] };
  try { V = Object.assign(V, JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(VKEY)) || {}); } catch (e) {}
  if (!Array.isArray(V.favs)) V.favs = [];
  if (V.big) { V.text = 118; V.big = false; }          // old "Bigger text" switch becomes a size
  V.text = Math.max(80, Math.min(160, +V.text || 100));
  const siteSpeed = () => { const v = +(D.site && D.site.bgSpeed); return v > 0 ? v : 1; };
  const curSpeed = () => Math.max(.25, Math.min(3, V.speed == null ? siteSpeed() : (+V.speed || 1)));
  const saveV = () => { try { localStorage.setItem(VKEY, JSON.stringify(V)); } catch (e) {} };

  /* ---------- Styles ---------- */
  const css = document.createElement("style");
  css.setAttribute("data-ppzv", "");
  css.textContent = `
body.noanim, body.vnoanim { animation:none !important; }
body.vnoanim *, body.vnoanim *::before, body.vnoanim *::after { animation:none !important; transition:none !important; }
body.vnoguest #guestBox { display:none !important; }
html.vbig { font-size:118%; }
html.vbig body { font-size:1.08rem; }

  box-shadow:0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,.5); touch-action:manipulation; -webkit-tap-highlight-color:transparent; }

  max-height:calc(100vh - 100px); max-height:calc(100dvh - 100px - env(safe-area-inset-top, 0px));
  overflow:auto; -webkit-overflow-scrolling:touch; background:var(--panel); border:1px solid var(--accent); border-radius:14px; padding:14px;
  box-shadow:0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,.5); }
@media (max-width:480px) { #ppzSet { left:12px; width:auto; } }
@media (max-width:600px) { header { margin-top:42px; } }   /* keeps the title clear of the settings button */
#ppzSet[hidden] { display:none; }
body.editing #ppzGear, body.editing #ppzSet { display:none; }

#ppzSet .note { color:var(--muted); font-size:.75rem; margin-top:10px; }

#ppzSet .row button { flex:1; min-height:42px; border-radius:10px; border:1px solid var(--line);
  background:var(--panel2); color:var(--text); font:inherit; cursor:pointer; }
#ppzSet .row button.pri { background:var(--accent); border-color:var(--accent); color:#12081f; font-weight:600; }
.tile { position:relative; }
.ppz-star { position:absolute; top:6px; right:6px; z-index:2; width:38px; height:38px; border-radius:50%;
  border:none; background:rgba(0,0,0,.55); color:#fff; font-size:1.15rem; line-height:1; cursor:pointer;
  touch-action:manipulation; }
.ppz-star.on { color:#ffd84d; }`;
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* ---------- Apply visitor settings ---------- */
  function applyV() {
    document.body.classList.toggle("vnoanim", !V.anim);
    document.body.classList.toggle("vnoguest", !V.guest);
    const rs = document.documentElement.style;
    if (V.text !== 100) rs.fontSize = V.text + "%"; else rs.removeProperty("font-size");
    rs.setProperty("--spd", curSpeed()); window.__ppzSpeed = curSpeed();
  }
  window.__ppzApplyV = applyV;
  applyV();
  const bpage = renderPage;
  renderPage = function () { bpage.apply(this, arguments); applyV(); };

  /* ---------- Settings button + panel ---------- */
  const gear = document.createElement("button");
  gear.id = "ppzGear"; gear.type = "button"; gear.textContent = "⚙";
  gear.setAttribute("aria-label", "Settings"); gear.setAttribute("data-ppzv", "");
  const panel = document.createElement("div");
  panel.id = "ppzSet"; panel.hidden = true; panel.setAttribute("data-ppzv", "");
  document.body.append(gear, panel);

  function buildPanel() {
    const ownerOff = D.site && D.site.anim === false;
    const sysOff = matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;
    const guestAvail = $("guestBox") && !(D.site && D.site.guest === false);
    const animNote = ownerOff ? "Turned off for everyone" : sysOff ? "Off because of your device's reduce-motion setting" : "Moving background and effects";
    panel.innerHTML = `
      <h3>⚙ Settings</h3>
      <label><span>Animations<small>${animNote}</small></span>
        <input type="checkbox" id="ppzVAnim" ${V.anim && !ownerOff && !sysOff ? "checked" : ""} ${ownerOff || sysOff ? "disabled" : ""}></label>
      ${guestAvail ? `<label><span>Show guest canvas<small>The drawing area anyone can use</small></span>
        <input type="checkbox" id="ppzVGuest" ${V.guest ? "checked" : ""}></label>` : ""}
      <label><span>Text size<small id="ppzVTextV">${V.text}%</small></span>
        <input type="range" id="ppzVText" min="80" max="160" step="5" value="${V.text}"></label>
      <label><span>Background speed<small id="ppzVSpdV">${+curSpeed().toFixed(2)}×${V.speed == null ? " (site default)" : ""}</small></span>
        <input type="range" id="ppzVSpd" min="25" max="300" step="25" value="${Math.round(curSpeed() * 100)}"></label>
      <p class="note">Saved on this device only. Favorites: ${V.favs.length}</p>
      <div class="row"><button type="button" id="ppzVReset">Reset</button><button type="button" class="pri" id="ppzVDone">Done</button></div>`;
  }
  const openPanel = () => { buildPanel(); panel.hidden = false; };
  const closePanel = () => { panel.hidden = true; };
  gear.addEventListener("click", e => { e.stopPropagation(); panel.hidden ? openPanel() : closePanel(); });
  panel.addEventListener("change", e => {
    const id = e.target.id;
    if (id === "ppzVAnim") V.anim = e.target.checked;
    else if (id === "ppzVGuest") V.guest = e.target.checked;
    else return;
    saveV(); applyV();
  });
  panel.addEventListener("input", e => {
    const id = e.target.id;
    if (id === "ppzVText") { V.text = +e.target.value; $("ppzVTextV").textContent = V.text + "%"; }
    else if (id === "ppzVSpd") { V.speed = +e.target.value / 100; $("ppzVSpdV").textContent = V.speed + "×"; }
    else return;
    saveV(); applyV();
  });
  panel.addEventListener("click", e => {
    if (e.target.id === "ppzVDone") closePanel();
    if (e.target.id === "ppzVReset") {
      if (!confirm("Reset your settings and remove all your favorites?")) return;
      V = { anim: true, guest: true, big: false, text: 100, speed: null, favs: [] };
      saveV(); applyV(); refreshStars(); renderFavs(); buildPanel();
    }
  });
  document.addEventListener("click", e => {
    if (!panel.hidden && !panel.contains(e.target) && e.target !== gear) closePanel();
  });
  document.addEventListener("keydown", e => { if (e.key === "Escape") closePanel(); });

  /* ---------- Favorites ---------- */
  const topBox = $("top5") && $("top5").closest(".box");
  let favGrid = null;
  if (topBox) {
    const box = document.createElement("div");
    box.className = "box"; box.id = "ppzFavBox"; box.hidden = true; box.setAttribute("data-ppzv", "");
    box.innerHTML = `<h2>⭐ Your favorites</h2><p class="sub">Only you can see these. Saved on this device.</p><div class="grid" id="ppzFavs"></div>`;
    topBox.before(box);
    favGrid = box.querySelector("#ppzFavs");
  }
  function renderFavs() {
    if (!favGrid) return;
    const list = D.proxies.map((p, i) => [p, i]).filter(([p]) => V.favs.includes(p.name));
    const keep = tileAnim; tileAnim = false;
    favGrid.innerHTML = list.map(([p, i]) => tile(p, i)).join("");
    tileAnim = keep;
    $("ppzFavBox").hidden = !list.length;
    addStars(favGrid);
  }
  function addStars(root) {
    root.querySelectorAll(".tile").forEach(t => {
      if (t.querySelector(".ppz-star")) return;
      const p = D.proxies[+t.dataset.i]; if (!p) return;
      const b = document.createElement("button");
      b.type = "button"; b.className = "ppz-star"; b.dataset.act = "ppzFav"; b.dataset.name = p.name;
      b.setAttribute("data-ppzv", "");
      t.appendChild(b);
    });
    refreshStars();
  }
  function refreshStars() {
    document.querySelectorAll(".ppz-star").forEach(b => {
      const on = V.favs.includes(b.dataset.name);
      b.classList.toggle("on", on); b.textContent = on ? "★" : "☆";
      b.setAttribute("aria-label", on ? "Remove from favorites" : "Add to favorites");
    });
  }
  ACT.ppzFav = el => {
    const n = el.dataset.name, on = V.favs.includes(n);
    V.favs = on ? V.favs.filter(x => x !== n) : [...V.favs, n];
    saveV(); refreshStars(); renderFavs();
    toast(on ? "Removed from favorites" : "⭐ Added to favorites");
  };
  ["top5", "all"].forEach(id => {
    const el = $(id); if (!el) return;
    new MutationObserver(() => { addStars(el); if (id === "all") renderFavs(); }).observe(el, { childList: true });
    addStars(el);
  });
  renderFavs();

  /* ---------- Backup / Restore (developer panel only) ---------- */
  if (typeof PUBLIC_BUILD !== "undefined" && !PUBLIC_BUILD && typeof renderDev === "function") {
    const baseRenderDev = renderDev;
    renderDev = function () { baseRenderDev.apply(this, arguments); addBackup(); };
    function addBackup() {
      const body = $("devBody"), dz = body && body.querySelector('[data-act="discard"]');
      if (!dz || body.querySelector("#ppzBackup")) return;
      const pics = Object.values(D.images || {}).filter(v => String(v).startsWith("data:"));
      const picKB = Math.round(pics.reduce((n, v) => n + v.length, 0) / 1024);
      const allKB = Math.round(JSON.stringify(D).length / 1024);
      const sec = document.createElement("div"); sec.id = "ppzBackup";
      sec.innerHTML = `<h3>💾 Backup</h3>
        <p class="sub">Saves all your proxies, pictures, canvas and settings into one file. Restore loads it back on any device. Your login is not included.</p>
        <p class="sub">Your data: ${allKB} KB · pictures stored inside the file: ${picKB} KB${picKB > 300 ? " (consider moving pictures into an images folder on GitHub)" : ""}</p>
        <button class="btn full" data-act="ppzBackup" style="margin-bottom:8px">⬇ Download backup</button>
        <button class="btn full" data-act="ppzRestore">⬆ Restore from backup</button>`;
      const h = dz.previousElementSibling;
      (h && h.tagName === "H3" ? h : dz).before(sec);
    }
    ACT.ppzBackup = () => {
      const { devHash, ...rest } = D;
      const text = JSON.stringify({ ppzBackup: 1, saved: new Date().toISOString(), data: rest }, null, 2);
      const a = document.createElement("a");
      a.href = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([text], { type: "application/json" }));
      a.download = "ProxyPathz-backup-" + new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10) + ".json";
      document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove();
      setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href), 5000);
      toast("Downloading backup…");
    };
    ACT.ppzRestore = () => {
      const inp = document.createElement("input");
      inp.type = "file"; inp.accept = ".json,application/json";
      inp.onchange = () => {
        const f = inp.files[0]; if (!f) return;
        const r = new FileReader();
        r.onload = () => {
          let d;
          try { const j = JSON.parse(r.result); d = j && j.data ? j.data : j; } catch (e) { d = null; }
          if (!d || !Array.isArray(d.proxies) || !d.site) return toast("That file isn't a ProxyPathz backup");
          if (!confirm(`Replace everything in the editor with this backup (${d.proxies.length} proxies)?`)) return;
          d.images = d.images || {};
          d.devHash = D.devHash;
          D = normalize(d);
          if (typeof selId !== "undefined") selId = null;
          save(); renderPage(); renderDev();
          if (typeof renderEd === "function") renderEd();
          renderFavs();
          toast("Backup restored");
        };
        r.readAsText(f);
      };
      inp.click();
    };
  }

  /* ---------- Keep all of this out of exported files ---------- */
  if (typeof buildHTML === "function") {
    const baseBuild = buildHTML;
    buildHTML = function () {
      const moved = [...document.querySelectorAll("[data-ppzv]")].map(e => [e, e.parentNode, e.nextSibling]);
      moved.forEach(([e]) => e.remove());
      const b = document.body.classList, h = document.documentElement.classList;
      const had = ["vnoanim", "vnoguest"].filter(c => b.contains(c)), big = h.contains("vbig");
      had.forEach(c => b.remove(c)); h.remove("vbig");
      const rs = document.documentElement.style, fs = rs.fontSize, spd = rs.getPropertyValue("--spd");
      rs.removeProperty("font-size"); rs.removeProperty("--spd");
      try { return baseBuild.apply(this, arguments); }
      finally {
        moved.reverse().forEach(([e, p, n]) => p.insertBefore(e, n && n.parentNode === p ? n : null));
        had.forEach(c => b.add(c)); if (big) h.add("vbig");
        if (fs) rs.fontSize = fs; if (spd) rs.setProperty("--spd", spd);
      }
    };
  }
})();
</script>
<script id="ppz-addon-background">
/* ===== ProxyPathz add-on: animated backgrounds + background pictures ===== */
(() => {
  if (window.__ppzBg) return; window.__ppzBg = true;

    catch (e) { toast("That picture is too big for this device. Try a smaller one."); return false; }
  };
  const siteBg = () => (D.site.bg && typeof D.site.bg === "object") ? D.site.bg : {};
  const motionOff = () => document.body.classList.contains("noanim") || document.body.classList.contains("vnoanim") ||
    matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;

  /* ---------- Styles ---------- */
  document.getElementById("ppzBgCss")?.remove();
  const css = document.createElement("style");
  css.id = "ppzBgCss"; css.setAttribute("data-ppzv", "");

html.ppz-bgon body { animation:none !important; background-image:none !important; }
#ppzBg { position:fixed; inset:0; z-index:-1; pointer-events:none; overflow:hidden; display:none; background:var(--bg); }

#ppzBg.has-pic .dim { display:block; }

#ppzBg canvas { display:none; width:100%; height:100%; }
#ppzBg.s-stars canvas { display:block; }
@keyframes ppzZoom { to { transform:scale(1.08); } }

#ppzBg.s-grid .fx { display:block; background-image:linear-gradient(var(--line) 1px,transparent 1px),
  linear-gradient(90deg,var(--line) 1px,transparent 1px); background-size:40px 40px; opacity:.6; animation:drift 20s linear infinite; }

#ppzBg.s-aurora .fx { display:block; background-image:linear-gradient(rgba(58,34,102,.35) 1px,transparent 1px),
  linear-gradient(90deg,rgba(58,34,102,.35) 1px,transparent 1px); background-size:40px 40px; }
#ppzBg.s-aurora .fx::before, #ppzBg.s-aurora .fx::after { content:""; position:absolute; width:70vmax; height:70vmax;
  border-radius:50%; will-change:transform; }
#ppzBg.s-aurora .fx::before { left:-20vmax; top:-25vmax; opacity:.55;
  background:radial-gradient(circle,var(--accent) 0%,transparent 62%); animation:ppzFloatA 22s ease-in-out infinite alternate; }
#ppzBg.s-aurora .fx::after { right:-25vmax; bottom:-30vmax; opacity:.35;
  background:radial-gradient(circle,#ff5cc8 0%,transparent 60%); animation:ppzFloatB 28s ease-in-out infinite alternate; }
@keyframes ppzFloatA { to { transform:translate(30vw,25vh) scale(1.2); } }
@keyframes ppzFloatB { to { transform:translate(-35vw,-20vh) scale(.85); } }

#ppzBg.s-neon .fx { display:block; background:linear-gradient(to bottom,var(--bg) 0%,#2b0f52 49.5%,var(--accent) 50%,var(--bg) 50.6%); }
#ppzBg.s-neon .fx::before { content:""; position:absolute; left:50%; top:50%; width:44vmin; height:44vmin; border-radius:50%;

#ppzBg.s-neon .fx::after { content:""; position:absolute; left:-50%; width:200%; top:50%; height:100%;
  background-image:linear-gradient(var(--accent) 2px,transparent 2px),linear-gradient(90deg,var(--accent) 2px,transparent 2px);

body { animation-duration:calc(20s / var(--spd, 1)); }
#ppzBg .pic { animation-duration:calc(40s / var(--spd, 1)); }
#ppzBg.s-grid .fx { animation-duration:calc(20s / var(--spd, 1)); }
#ppzBg.s-aurora .fx::before { animation-duration:calc(22s / var(--spd, 1)); }
#ppzBg.s-aurora .fx::after { animation-duration:calc(28s / var(--spd, 1)); }
#ppzBg.s-neon .fx::after { animation-duration:calc(1.6s / var(--spd, 1)); }
#ppzSet select { background:var(--panel2); color:var(--text); border:1px solid var(--line); border-radius:8px;
  padding:8px; font:inherit; font-size:16px; max-width:150px; }
#ppzSet input[type=range] { width:130px; accent-color:var(--accent); }
#ppzSet .row button:disabled { opacity:.4; cursor:default; }
#ppzBgOwner select { width:100%; padding:9px; border-radius:8px; border:1px solid var(--line);
  background:var(--panel2); color:var(--text); font:inherit; font-size:16px; }
#ppzBgOwner input[type=range] { width:100%; accent-color:var(--accent); }`;
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* ---------- Background layer ---------- */
  document.getElementById("ppzBg")?.remove();
  const layer = document.createElement("div");
  layer.id = "ppzBg"; layer.setAttribute("data-ppzv", ""); layer.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
  layer.innerHTML = '<div class="pic"></div><div class="dim"></div><div class="fx"></div><canvas></canvas>';
  document.body.prepend(layer);
  const pic = layer.querySelector(".pic"), dim = layer.querySelector(".dim");
  const cv = layer.querySelector("canvas"), ctx = cv.getContext("2d");

  /* ---------- Starfield ---------- */
  let stars = [], shoot = null, raf = 0, last = 0, W = 0, H = 0, starsOn = false, wasOff = null, starColor = "#a66bff";
  function sizeStars() {
    const dpr = Math.min(devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
    W = innerWidth; H = innerHeight;
    cv.width = W * dpr; cv.height = H * dpr; ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
    const n = Math.min(220, Math.round(W * H / 5000));
    stars = Array.from({ length: n }, () => ({ x: Math.random() * W, y: Math.random() * H, r: Math.random() * 1.4 + .3,
      p: Math.random() * 6.28, s: Math.random() * 1.5 + .5, v: Math.random() * .15 + .03 }));
  }
  function drawStars(t, move) {
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
    for (const s of stars) {
      if (move) { s.y += s.v * SP(); if (s.y > H) { s.y = 0; s.x = Math.random() * W; } }
      ctx.globalAlpha = .35 + .65 * Math.abs(Math.sin(s.p + t / 1000 * s.s * SP()));
      ctx.fillStyle = s.r > 1.35 ? starColor : "#fff";
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, s.r, 0, 6.283); ctx.fill();
    }
    if (!move) return;
    if (!shoot && Math.random() < .004 * SP()) shoot = { x: Math.random() * W * .7, y: Math.random() * H * .4, life: 1 };
    if (shoot) {
      const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(shoot.x, shoot.y, shoot.x - 90, shoot.y - 40);
      g.addColorStop(0, "rgba(255,255,255," + shoot.life + ")"); g.addColorStop(1, "rgba(255,255,255,0)");
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1; ctx.strokeStyle = g; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(shoot.x, shoot.y); ctx.lineTo(shoot.x - 90, shoot.y - 40); ctx.stroke();
      shoot.x += 10 * SP(); shoot.y += 4.5 * SP(); shoot.life -= .03 * SP();
      if (shoot.life <= 0) shoot = null;
    }
  }
  function loop(t) { raf = requestAnimationFrame(loop); if (t - last < 33) return; last = t; drawStars(t, true); }
  function stopStars() { cancelAnimationFrame(raf); raf = 0; }
  function startStars() {
    stopStars(); sizeStars(); wasOff = motionOff();
    if (wasOff || document.hidden) { drawStars(0, false); return; }
    raf = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
  }
  document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", () => {
    if (!starsOn) return;
    if (document.hidden) stopStars(); else if (!motionOff() && !raf) raf = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
  });
  let rz = 0;
  addEventListener("resize", () => {
    if (!starsOn) return;
    clearTimeout(rz);
    rz = setTimeout(() => { if (Math.abs(innerWidth - W) > 2 || Math.abs(innerHeight - H) > 120) startStars(); }, 250);
  });
  new MutationObserver(() => { if (starsOn && motionOff() !== wasOff) startStars(); })
    .observe(document.body, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ["class"] });

  /* ---------- Apply ---------- */
  let curImg = null;
  function effective() {
    const sb = siteBg();
    let style = VB.style && VB.style !== "default" ? VB.style : (sb.style || "grid");
    if (!OK.includes(style)) style = "grid";
    const img = VB.img || sb.img || "";
    const d = VB.img ? VB.dim : (sb.dim ?? 45);
    return { style, img, dim: Math.max(0, Math.min(85, +d || 0)) };
  }
  function applyBg() {
    const e = effective(), on = !!e.img || e.style !== "grid";
    layer.className = on ? "on s-" + e.style + (e.img ? " has-pic" : "") : "";
    document.documentElement.classList.toggle("ppz-bgon", on);
    if (e.img !== curImg) { pic.style.backgroundImage = e.img ? 'url("' + e.img.replace(/"/g, "%22") + '")' : ""; curImg = e.img; }
    dim.style.opacity = e.img ? e.dim / 100 : 0;
    starColor = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue("--accent").trim() || "#a66bff";
    const want = on && e.style === "stars";
    if (want && !starsOn) { starsOn = true; startStars(); }
    else if (!want && starsOn) { starsOn = false; stopStars(); }
  }
  const basePage = renderPage;
  renderPage = function () { basePage.apply(this, arguments); applyBg(); };
  applyBg();

  /* ---------- Picture helpers ---------- */
  function pickFile(cb) {
    const inp = document.createElement("input");
    inp.type = "file"; inp.accept = "image/*";
    inp.onchange = () => inp.files[0] && cb(inp.files[0]);
    inp.click();
  }
  function readPic(file, max) {
    return new Promise((res, rej) => {
      if (!file.type.startsWith("image/")) return rej("not an image");
      const fr = new FileReader();
      fr.onerror = rej;
      fr.onload = () => {
        if (file.type === "image/gif" && file.size < 1.5e6) return res(fr.result);
        const im = new Image();
        im.onload = () => {
          const k = Math.min(1, max / Math.max(im.width, im.height));
          const c = document.createElement("canvas");
          c.width = Math.round(im.width * k); c.height = Math.round(im.height * k);
          c.getContext("2d").drawImage(im, 0, 0, c.width, c.height);
          res(c.toDataURL("image/jpeg", .8));
        };
        im.onerror = rej;
        im.src = fr.result;
      };
      fr.readAsDataURL(file);
    });
  }
  const opt = (sel) => STYLES.map(([v, l]) => `<option value="${v}" ${sel === v ? "selected" : ""}>${l}</option>`).join("");

  /* ---------- Visitor: ⚙ Settings section ---------- */
  const panel = $("ppzSet");
  function addVisitor() {
    if (!panel || !panel.lastElementChild || panel.querySelector("#ppzVBgSec")) return;
    const sec = document.createElement("div");
    sec.id = "ppzVBgSec";
    sec.innerHTML = `<h3 style="margin-top:12px">🌌 Background</h3>
      <label><span>Style<small>Just for you</small></span>
        <select id="ppzVStyle"><option value="default" ${VB.style === "default" ? "selected" : ""}>Site default</option>${opt(VB.style)}</select></label>
      <div class="row" style="margin-top:8px">
        <button type="button" id="ppzVUp">⬆ My picture</button>
        <button type="button" id="ppzVDel" ${VB.img ? "" : "disabled"}>Remove picture</button></div>
      ${VB.img ? `<label><span>Darken picture<small id="ppzVDimV">${VB.dim}%</small></span>
        <input type="range" id="ppzVDim" min="0" max="85" value="${VB.dim}"></label>` : ""}`;
    panel.insertBefore(sec, panel.lastElementChild);
    const redo = () => { sec.remove(); addVisitor(); };
    $("ppzVStyle").onchange = e => { VB.style = e.target.value; saveVB(); applyBg(); };
    $("ppzVUp").onclick = () => pickFile(async f => {
      try {
        const old = VB.img; VB.img = await readPic(f, 1400);
        if (!saveVB()) VB.img = old;
        applyBg(); redo();
      } catch (e) { toast("Couldn't read that picture"); }
    });
    $("ppzVDel").onclick = () => { VB.img = ""; saveVB(); applyBg(); redo(); };
    const dr = $("ppzVDim");
    if (dr) {
      dr.oninput = () => { VB.dim = +dr.value; $("ppzVDimV").textContent = dr.value + "%"; applyBg(); };
      dr.onchange = saveVB;
    }
  }
  if (panel) { new MutationObserver(addVisitor).observe(panel, { childList: true }); addVisitor(); }

  /* ---------- Owner: Look tab section ---------- */
  if (typeof PUBLIC_BUILD !== "undefined" && !PUBLIC_BUILD && typeof renderDev === "function") {
    const escA = s => String(s).replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/"/g, "&quot;").replace(/</g, "&lt;");
    const baseDev = renderDev;
    renderDev = function () { baseDev.apply(this, arguments); addOwner(); };

    function addOwner() {
      const body = $("devBody");
      if (!body || !body.querySelector('[data-path="site.accent"]') || body.querySelector("#ppzBgOwner")) return;
      const sb = siteBg(), d = sb.dim ?? 45, img = sb.img || "";
      const isData = img.startsWith("data:");
      const osp = +D.site.bgSpeed > 0 ? +D.site.bgSpeed : 1;
      const info = !img ? "No picture yet."
        : isData ? `Uploaded picture is stored inside your file (${Math.round(img.length / 1024)} KB).`
        : "Using a picture link.";
      const sec = document.createElement("div");
      sec.id = "ppzBgOwner";
      sec.innerHTML = `
        <h3 style="margin-top:18px">🌌 Background</h3>
        <p class="sub">This is what everyone sees. Visitors can still pick their own in ⚙ Settings.</p>
        <label class="field"><span>Animated style</span>
          <select id="ppzOStyle">${opt(sb.style || "grid")}</select></label>
        <label class="field"><span>Animation speed: <b id="ppzOSpdV">${+osp.toFixed(2)}×</b></span>
          <input type="range" id="ppzOSpd" min="25" max="300" step="25" value="${Math.round(osp * 100)}"></label>
        <label class="field"><span>Picture link (best option, keeps your file small)</span>
          <input id="ppzOLink" placeholder="images/background.jpg" value="${isData ? "" : escA(img)}"></label>
        <div class="row" style="margin:8px 0">
          <button type="button" class="btn sm" id="ppzOUp">⬆ Upload picture</button>
          <button type="button" class="btn sm danger" id="ppzODel" ${img ? "" : "disabled"}>Remove picture</button></div>
        <p class="sub">${info}</p>
        ${img ? `<label class="field"><span>Darken picture: <b id="ppzODimV">${d}%</b></span>
          <input type="range" id="ppzODim" min="0" max="85" value="${d}"></label>` : ""}`;
      body.appendChild(sec);

      const redraw = () => { sec.remove(); addOwner(); };
      const setSite = patch => { D.site.bg = Object.assign({}, siteBg(), patch); save(); applyBg(); };

      $("ppzOStyle").onchange = e => setSite({ style: e.target.value });
      const sd = $("ppzOSpd");
      sd.oninput = () => { D.site.bgSpeed = +sd.value / 100; $("ppzOSpdV").textContent = D.site.bgSpeed + "×"; window.__ppzApplyV && window.__ppzApplyV(); };
      sd.onchange = () => save();
      $("ppzOLink").onchange = e => { setSite({ img: e.target.value.trim() }); redraw(); };
      $("ppzOUp").onclick = () => pickFile(async f => {
        try {
          const data = await readPic(f, 1600);
          setSite({ img: data });
          toast("Background set (" + Math.round(data.length / 1024) + " KB added to your file)");
          redraw();
        } catch (e) { toast("Couldn't read that picture"); }
      });
      $("ppzODel").onclick = () => { setSite({ img: "" }); redraw(); };
      const dr = $("ppzODim");
      if (dr) {
        dr.oninput = () => {
          $("ppzODimV").textContent = dr.value + "%";
          D.site.bg = Object.assign({}, siteBg(), { dim: +dr.value });
          applyBg();
        };
        dr.onchange = () => save();
      }
    }
    addOwner();
  }
})();
</script>
<script id="ppz-addon-cards">
/* ===== ProxyPathz add-on: custom card title bars ===== */
(() => {
  if (window.__ppzCards) return; window.__ppzCards = true;

    ["animated", "Animated gradient"], ["neon", "Neon glow"], ["shine", "Shine"], ["picture", "Picture"], ["none", "None (text only)"]];

  const KEYS = ["capStyle", "capC1", "capC2", "capText", "capImage"];

  /* ---------- Styles ---------- */
  document.getElementById("ppzCardsCss")?.remove();
  const css = document.createElement("style");
  css.id = "ppzCardsCss"; css.setAttribute("data-ppzv", "");

.tile .info.cs-glass { background:rgba(29,15,51,.42) !important; backdrop-filter:blur(10px); -webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(10px);
  border-top:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.14); }
.tile .info.cs-solid { background:var(--c1) !important; }
.tile .info.cs-gradient { background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--c1),var(--c2)) !important; }

  background-size:300% 100% !important; animation:ppzCapFlow 6s ease-in-out infinite; }

  box-shadow:0 -6px 18px -6px var(--c1); }
.tile .info.cs-neon h3 { color:#fff; text-shadow:0 0 6px var(--c1),0 0 14px var(--c1); }
.tile .info.cs-shine { background:linear-gradient(110deg,transparent 35%,rgba(255,255,255,.28) 50%,transparent 65%),
  rgba(29,15,51,.5) !important; background-size:250% 100%,auto !important; backdrop-filter:blur(8px);
  -webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(8px); animation:ppzCapShine 3.5s linear infinite; }
@keyframes ppzCapShine { from { background-position:150% 0,0 0; } to { background-position:-50% 0,0 0; } }

.tile .info.ct-light h3, .tile .info.ct-light small { color:#fff !important; }
.tile .info.ct-dark h3, .tile .info.ct-dark small { color:#12081f !important; text-shadow:none !important; }
.ppzCapPrev { pointer-events:none; max-width:230px; margin:8px 0 14px; }
.ppzCapPrev .tile { animation:none !important; }`;
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* ---------- Add the style to every card ---------- */
  const baseTile = tile;
  tile = function (p, i) {
    const html = baseTile.apply(this, arguments);
    if (!p) return html;
    const S = D.site || {};
    let s = p.capStyle || S.capStyle || "default";
    if (!CAP_OK.includes(s)) s = "default";
    const c1 = p.capC1 || S.capC1 || "#a66bff";
    const c2 = p.capC2 || S.capC2 || "#2b6bff";
    const txt = p.capText || S.capText || "auto";
    let vars = `--c1:${c1};--c2:${c2};`;
    if (s === "picture") {
      const src = img(p.capImage || S.capImage || "");
      if (src) vars += `--ci:url("${src}");`; else s = "glass";
    }
    return html.replace('<div class="info">', `<div class="info cs-${s} ct-${txt}" style="${esc(vars)}">`);
  };

  /* ---------- Developer panel controls ---------- */
  if (typeof PUBLIC_BUILD !== "undefined" && PUBLIC_BUILD) { renderPage(); return; }
  const picOpts = () => [["", "(none)"], ...Object.keys(D.images || {}).map(k => [k, k])];

  function fields(base, o, isProxy) {
    const S = D.site || {};
    const styleList = isProxy ? [["", "Use site style"], ...CAPS] : CAPS;
    const txtList = isProxy ? [["", "Use site text color"], ...TXT] : TXT;
    const c1 = o.capC1 || S.capC1 || "#a66bff", c2 = o.capC2 || S.capC2 || "#2b6bff";
    return `
      <label class="field"><span>Style</span>
        <select data-path="${base}.capStyle">${opts(styleList, o.capStyle || (isProxy ? "" : "default"))}</select></label>
      <div class="row">
        <label class="field" style="flex:1"><span>Color 1</span>
          <input type="color" data-path="${base}.capC1" value="${esc(c1)}" style="height:44px;padding:4px"></label>
        <label class="field" style="flex:1"><span>Color 2</span>
          <input type="color" data-path="${base}.capC2" value="${esc(c2)}" style="height:44px;padding:4px"></label>
      </div>
      <label class="field"><span>Text color</span>
        <select data-path="${base}.capText">${opts(txtList, o.capText || (isProxy ? "" : "auto"))}</select></label>
      <label class="field"><span>Picture (for the "Picture" style)</span>
        <select data-path="${base}.capImage">${opts(picOpts(), o.capImage || "")}</select></label>
      <p class="sub">Colors are used by Solid, Gradient, Animated and Neon.</p>`;
  }

  function drawPreviews() {
    document.querySelectorAll("#devBody .ppzCapPrev").forEach(box => {
      const i = +box.dataset.i, p = D.proxies[i];
      if (!p) { box.innerHTML = `<p class="sub">Add a proxy to see a preview.</p>`; return; }
      const keep = tileAnim; tileAnim = false;
      box.innerHTML = `<div class="grid">${tile(p, i)}</div>`;
      tileAnim = keep;
    });
  }

  function addControls() {
    const body = $("devBody"); if (!body) return;
    if (tab === "look" && !body.querySelector("#ppzCapSite")) {
      const sec = document.createElement("div");
      sec.id = "ppzCapSite";
      sec.innerHTML = `<h3 style="margin-top:18px">🏷 Card title bars</h3>
        <p class="sub">The bar with the name and "click to see more" on every card. Each proxy can override this in the Proxies tab.</p>
        ${fields("site", D.site, false)}
        <div class="ppzCapPrev" data-i="0"></div>`;
      body.appendChild(sec);
    }
    if (tab === "proxies") {
      body.querySelectorAll("details.card[data-i]").forEach(card => {
        if (card.querySelector(".ppzCap")) return;
        const i = +card.dataset.i, p = D.proxies[i], inner = card.querySelector(".in");
        if (!p || !inner) return;
        const box = document.createElement("div");
        box.className = "ppzCap";
        box.innerHTML = `<h3>🏷 Title bar</h3>${fields("proxies." + i, p, true)}
          <div class="ppzCapPrev" data-i="${i}"></div>
          <button class="btn sm" data-act="ppzCapReset" data-i="${i}" style="margin-bottom:12px">Reset to site style</button>`;
        const links = [...inner.querySelectorAll("h3")].find(h => h.textContent.trim() === "Links");
        links ? inner.insertBefore(box, links) : inner.appendChild(box);
      });
    }
    drawPreviews();
  }

  ACT.ppzCapReset = el => {
    const p = D.proxies[+el.dataset.i]; if (!p) return;
    KEYS.forEach(k => delete p[k]);
    save(); renderDev(); renderPage();
    toast("Title bar reset");
  };

  const baseDev = renderDev;
  renderDev = function () { baseDev.apply(this, arguments); addControls(); };

  const onCapEdit = e => {
    const path = e.target.dataset && e.target.dataset.path;
    if (path && /\.cap(Style|C1|C2|Text|Image)$/.test(path)) setTimeout(drawPreviews, 0);
  };
  document.addEventListener("input", onCapEdit);
  document.addEventListener("change", onCapEdit);

  renderPage();
  if ($("dev").classList.contains("open")) renderDev();
})();
