<script id="ppz-addon-publish">
/* ===== ProxyPathz add-on: Publish to GitHub ===== */
(() => {
  if (window.__ppzPub) return; window.__ppzPub = true;
  if (typeof PUBLIC_BUILD !== "undefined" && PUBLIC_BUILD) return;

  const CKEY = "proxypathz_v15_pub", TKEY = "proxypathz_v15_pubtoken";
  let C = { owner: "christopher806", repo: "Proxy-Path", branch: "main", path: "index.html", sv: true, remember: false, last: 0 };
  try { C = Object.assign(C, JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(CKEY)) || {}); } catch (e) {}
  const saveC = () => { try { localStorage.setItem(CKEY, JSON.stringify(C)); } catch (e) {} };
  const getToken = () => sessionStorage.getItem(TKEY) || localStorage.getItem(TKEY) || "";
  function setToken(t, remember) {
    sessionStorage.removeItem(TKEY); localStorage.removeItem(TKEY);
    if (t) (remember ? localStorage : sessionStorage).setItem(TKEY, t);
  }

  /* ---------- GitHub API (uses XHR so leftover preview code can't block it) ---------- */
  function gh(method, url, body) {
    return new Promise((res, rej) => {
      const x = new XMLHttpRequest();
      x.open(method, "https://api.github.com" + url);
      x.setRequestHeader("Accept", "application/vnd.github+json");
      x.setRequestHeader("Authorization", "Bearer " + getToken());
      x.setRequestHeader("X-GitHub-Api-Version", "2022-11-28");
      if (body) x.setRequestHeader("Content-Type", "application/json");
      x.onload = () => { let j = null; try { j = JSON.parse(x.responseText); } catch (e) {} res({ status: x.status, json: j }); };
      x.onerror = () => rej(new Error("Network error. Check your internet."));
      x.send(body ? JSON.stringify(body) : null);
    });
  }
  const repoUrl = () => `/repos/${encodeURIComponent(C.owner)}/${encodeURIComponent(C.repo)}`;
  const fileUrl = p => `${repoUrl()}/contents/${p.split("/").map(encodeURIComponent).join("/")}`;
  function errText(s) {
    if (s === 401) return "Token is wrong or expired. Make a new one.";
    if (s === 403) return "Token can't write to this repo. Check Contents: Read and write.";
    if (s === 404) return "Repo, branch or file not found. Check the spelling.";
    if (s === 409 || s === 422) return "The file changed on GitHub at the same moment. Try again.";
    return "GitHub said error " + s + ".";
  }
  function toB64(str) {
    const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(str);
    let bin = "";
    for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += 0x8000) bin += String.fromCharCode.apply(null, bytes.subarray(i, i + 0x8000));
    return btoa(bin);
  }
  function fromB64(b) {
    try { return new TextDecoder().decode(Uint8Array.from(atob(b.replace(/\s/g, "")), c => c.charCodeAt(0))); }
    catch (e) { return ""; }
  }
  async function getSha(path) {
    const r = await gh("GET", fileUrl(path) + "?ref=" + encodeURIComponent(C.branch) + "&t=" + Date.now());
    if (r.status === 200) return { sha: r.json.sha, text: r.json.content ? fromB64(r.json.content) : "" };
    if (r.status === 404) return { sha: null, text: "" };
    throw new Error(errText(r.status));
  }
  async function putB64(path, b64, msg) {
    for (let tryNo = 0; tryNo < 2; tryNo++) {
      const cur = await getSha(path);
      const body = { message: msg, content: b64, branch: C.branch };
      if (cur.sha) body.sha = cur.sha;
      const r = await gh("PUT", fileUrl(path), body);
      if (r.status === 200 || r.status === 201) return;
      if ((r.status === 409 || r.status === 422) && tryNo === 0) continue;
      throw new Error(errText(r.status));
    }
  }
  const putFile = (path, text, msg) => putB64(path, toB64(text), msg);

  /* ---------- Build the file ---------- */
  const MARKS = ["real" + "Parent", "__cowork" + "HtmlReviewGuest", "window." + "claude"];
  /* Never remove the main app, the data, or our add-ons */
  const MAIN_SIG = "const PUBLIC" + "_BUILD", DATA_SIG = "const DA" + "TA", SET_SIG = "<script id=" + '"settings"';
  const isOurs = b => b.includes(MAIN_SIG) || b.startsWith(SET_SIG) || /^<script id="ppz-addon-/.test(b);
  function cleanHTML(html) {
    return html
      .replace(/<script\b[^>]*>[\s\S]*?<\/script>/gi, b =>
        !isOurs(b) && (MARKS.some(m => b.includes(m)) || b.includes("chrome-" + "extension://")) ? "" : b)
      .replace(/<style\b[^>]*>[\s\S]*?<\/style>/gi, b =>
        ["claude-" + "scrollbar", "__cwhr" + "-sel", "chrome-" + "extension://", "moz-" + "extension://"].some(m => b.includes(m)) ? "" : b);
  }
  /* Refuse to upload a broken file */
  function checkHTML(html) {
    const miss = [[MAIN_SIG, "main app code"], [DATA_SIG, "your data"], [SET_SIG, "settings block"]]
      .filter(([sig]) => !html.includes(sig)).map(([, name]) => name);
    if (miss.length) throw new Error("Stopped: the file is missing " + miss.join(", ") + ". Nothing was uploaded.");
  }
  const addonIds = html => [...html.matchAll(/<script id="(ppz-addon-[\w-]+)"/g)].map(m => m[1]);
  function sourceView(html) {
    const e = html.replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/</g, "&lt;").replace(/>/g, "&gt;");
    return '<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><meta charset="UTF-8"><title>source</title></head><body><pre>' + e + "</pre></body></html>";
  }

  /* ---------- Publish ---------- */
  let busy = false;
  async function publish() {
    if (busy) return;
    const token = getToken();
    if (!token) { toast("Add your GitHub token first"); openConn(); return; }
    if (!confirm("Publish your edits to the live website?\n\nThis replaces " + C.path + " on GitHub with what's in this editor.")) return;
    busy = true; setLog("⏳ Building file…");
    try {
      const html = cleanHTML(buildHTML(false));
      if (html.includes(token)) throw new Error("Stopped: your token ended up inside the page text. Remove it and try again.");
      checkHTML(html);
      setLog("⏳ Checking GitHub…");
      const remote = await getSha(C.path);
      if (remote.text) {
        const mine = addonIds(html), missing = addonIds(remote.text).filter(id => !mine.includes(id));
        if (missing.length && !confirm("GitHub's file has add-ons this page doesn't:\n" + missing.join("\n") +
          "\n\nPublishing now would REMOVE them. Press Cancel, reload the live site, then publish.\n\nPublish anyway?")) {
          setLog("Cancelled. Reload the live site and try again."); busy = false; return;
        }
      }
      if (remote.text && html.length < remote.text.length * 0.6 &&
          !confirm("The new file is MUCH smaller than the one on GitHub (" + Math.round(html.length / 1024) + " KB vs " +
            Math.round(remote.text.length / 1024) + " KB). Something might be missing.\n\nPublish anyway?")) {
        setLog("Cancelled. Nothing was uploaded."); busy = false; return;
      }
      const msg = "Update from ProxyPathz editor (" + new Date().toLocaleString() + ")";
      setLog("⏳ Uploading " + C.path + "…");
      await putFile(C.path, html, msg);
      if (C.sv) { setLog("⏳ Uploading source-view.html…"); await putFile("source-view.html", sourceView(html), msg); }
      C.last = Date.now(); saveC();
      setLog("✅ Published! The live site updates in about a minute.");
      toast("🚀 Published to GitHub");
    } catch (e) {
      setLog("❌ " + e.message);
    }
    busy = false;
  }

  /* ---------- UI in the developer panel ---------- */
  const escA = s => String(s).replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/"/g, "&quot;").replace(/</g, "&lt;");
  function setLog(t) { const el = $("ppzPubLog"); if (el) el.textContent = t; }
  function openConn() { const d = $("ppzPubConn"); if (d) d.open = true; }
  const lastText = () => C.last ? "Last published: " + new Date(C.last).toLocaleString() : "Not published from this device yet.";

  function addPublish() {
    const body = $("devBody");
    if (!body || !body.querySelector('[data-act="discard"]') || body.querySelector("#ppzPub")) return;
    const has = !!getToken();
    const sec = document.createElement("div");
    sec.id = "ppzPub";
    sec.style.cssText = "border:1px solid var(--accent);border-radius:12px;padding:12px;margin-bottom:16px;background:var(--panel2)";
    sec.innerHTML = `
      <h3 style="margin-top:0">🚀 Publish to GitHub</h3>
      <p class="sub">Puts your edits on the real website for everyone. ${lastText()}</p>
      <button type="button" class="btn full" id="ppzPubGo"
        style="background:var(--accent);color:#12081f;font-weight:700;min-height:48px">🚀 Publish now</button>
      <p class="sub" id="ppzPubLog" style="margin-top:8px">${has ? "Ready." : "Connect GitHub below first."}</p>
      <details id="ppzUpBox" style="margin-top:8px">
        <summary style="cursor:pointer;padding:8px 0">📁 Upload a file to GitHub</summary>
        <p class="sub">Pick a file from your device, like an updated ProxyPathz.html or a picture. To replace the live site, pick the HTML file and keep the name below as index.html.</p>
        <input type="file" id="ppzUpFile" style="margin-bottom:8px;width:100%">
        <label class="field"><span>Save it on GitHub as</span><input id="ppzUpPath" value="${escA(C.path)}" placeholder="index.html or images/photo.png"></label>
        <button type="button" class="btn full" id="ppzUpGo">⬆ Upload file</button>
      </details>
      <details id="ppzPubConn" ${has ? "" : "open"} style="margin-top:8px">
        <summary style="cursor:pointer;padding:8px 0">⚙ GitHub connection ${has ? "✅" : "(not connected)"}</summary>
        <label class="field"><span>Token (starts with github_pat_)</span>
          <input type="password" id="ppzPubTok" autocomplete="off" placeholder="${has ? "Saved. Paste a new one to replace it" : "Paste token here"}"></label>
        <label style="display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;margin:6px 0 10px">
          <input type="checkbox" id="ppzPubRem" ${C.remember ? "checked" : ""} style="width:22px;height:22px">
          <span>Remember on this device <small style="color:var(--muted)">(only on your own device)</small></span></label>
        <div class="row">
          <label class="field" style="flex:1"><span>Username</span><input id="ppzPubOwner" value="${escA(C.owner)}"></label>
          <label class="field" style="flex:1"><span>Repo</span><input id="ppzPubRepo" value="${escA(C.repo)}"></label>
        </div>
        <div class="row">
          <label class="field" style="flex:1"><span>Branch</span><input id="ppzPubBranch" value="${escA(C.branch)}"></label>
          <label class="field" style="flex:1"><span>File</span><input id="ppzPubPath" value="${escA(C.path)}"></label>
        </div>
        <label style="display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;margin:6px 0 10px">
          <input type="checkbox" id="ppzPubSv" ${C.sv ? "checked" : ""} style="width:22px;height:22px">
          <span>Also update source-view.html</span></label>
        <div class="row">
          <button type="button" class="btn sm" id="ppzPubSave">💾 Save</button>
          <button type="button" class="btn sm" id="ppzPubTest">🔌 Test</button>
          <button type="button" class="btn sm danger" id="ppzPubForget">Forget token</button>
        </div>
      </details>`;
    body.prepend(sec);

    const readForm = () => {
      C.owner = $("ppzPubOwner").value.trim(); C.repo = $("ppzPubRepo").value.trim();
      C.branch = $("ppzPubBranch").value.trim() || "main"; C.path = $("ppzPubPath").value.trim() || "index.html";
      C.sv = $("ppzPubSv").checked; C.remember = $("ppzPubRem").checked;
      const t = $("ppzPubTok").value.trim();
      setToken(t || getToken(), C.remember);
      $("ppzPubTok").value = "";
      saveC();
    };
    $("ppzPubGo").onclick = publish;
    $("ppzUpFile").onchange = () => {
      const f = $("ppzUpFile").files[0]; if (!f) return;
      $("ppzUpPath").value = /\.html?$/i.test(f.name) ? C.path : "images/" + f.name.replace(/[^\w.\-]/g, "_");
    };
    $("ppzUpGo").onclick = async () => {
      const f = $("ppzUpFile").files[0], path = $("ppzUpPath").value.trim().replace(/^\/+/, "");
      if (!getToken()) { toast("Add your GitHub token first"); openConn(); return; }
      if (!f || !path) return setLog("Choose a file first.");
      if (f.size > 25 * 1024 * 1024) return setLog("That file is over 25 MB, too big to upload this way.");
      if (!confirm("Upload " + f.name + " to " + C.owner + "/" + C.repo + " as " + path + "?\n\nIf that file already exists it will be replaced.")) return;
      setLog("⏳ Reading file…");
      try {
        const b64 = await new Promise((res, rej) => {
          const r = new FileReader(); r.onerror = rej;
          r.onload = () => res(String(r.result).split(",")[1] || ""); r.readAsDataURL(f);
        });
        setLog("⏳ Uploading " + path + "…");
        await putB64(path, b64, "Upload " + path + " from ProxyPathz editor");
        setLog("✅ Uploaded " + path + ". The live site updates in about a minute.");
        toast("🚀 File uploaded");
      } catch (e) { setLog("❌ " + e.message); }
    };
    $("ppzPubSave").onclick = () => { readForm(); toast("Connection saved"); sec.remove(); addPublish(); };
    $("ppzPubTest").onclick = async () => {
      readForm();
      if (!getToken()) return setLog("Paste your token first.");
      setLog("⏳ Testing…");
      try { await getSha(C.path); setLog("✅ Connected! Found " + C.path + " in " + C.owner + "/" + C.repo + "."); }
      catch (e) { setLog("❌ " + e.message); }
    };
    $("ppzPubForget").onclick = () => {
      if (!confirm("Forget the GitHub token on this device?")) return;
      setToken("", false); toast("Token forgotten"); sec.remove(); addPublish();
    };
  }

  const baseDev = renderDev;
  renderDev = function () { baseDev.apply(this, arguments); addPublish(); };
  if ($("dev").classList.contains("open")) renderDev();
})();
</script>
<script id="ppz-addon-pageedit">
/* ===== ProxyPathz add-on: Page Editor (move, hide, edit text, add shapes anywhere) ===== */
(() => {
  if (window.__ppzPE) return; window.__ppzPE = true;
  const FONTS = [["", "Default"], ["Poppins", "Poppins"], ["Orbitron", "Orbitron"], ["Russo One", "Russo One"],
    ["Space Grotesk", "Space Grotesk"], ["JetBrains Mono", "JetBrains Mono"]];
  const NAMES = { rect: "▭ Rectangle", circle: "◯ Circle", text: "🔤 Text", img: "🖼 Picture", btn: "🔗 Link button" };

  const DEV = typeof PUBLIC_BUILD === "undefined" || !PUBLIC_BUILD;
  const norm = s => String(s || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  const clamp = (v, a, b) => Math.max(a, Math.min(b, v));
  const r1 = v => Math.round(v * 10) / 10;
  const S = () => D.site;
  const lay = () => S().pageLayout || {};
  const layW = () => (S().pageLayout ||= {});

  const shapesW = () => Array.isArray(S().overlay) ? S().overlay : (S().overlay = []);
  const safeHref = h => { const u = String(h || "").trim(); return !u || /^\s*(javascript|data|vbscript):/i.test(u) ? "#" : u; };

  /* ---------- Styles ---------- */
  document.getElementById("ppzPECss")?.remove();
  const css = document.createElement("style");
  css.id = "ppzPECss"; css.setAttribute("data-ppzv", "");
  css.textContent = `
.ppz-hid { display:none !important; }

  overflow:hidden; background-position:center; background-repeat:no-repeat; line-height:1.2; padding:2px; word-break:break-word; }
.ppz-ov.ppz-btn { text-decoration:none; font-weight:600; cursor:pointer; box-shadow:0 4px 14px rgba(0,0,0,.35); }
.ppz-ov.ppz-text { background:transparent; text-shadow:0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,.5); }
body.ppz-pe { padding-top:64px; padding-bottom:46vh; }
body.ppz-pe [data-ppzk] { outline:1px dashed rgba(166,107,255,.45); outline-offset:4px; }
body.ppz-pe .ppz-selpage { outline:2px solid #4dd2ff !important; outline-offset:2px; touch-action:none; cursor:move; }
body.ppz-pe .ppz-hidE { opacity:.35; outline:2px dashed #ff5c7a !important; }
body.ppz-pe .ppz-ov { outline:1px dashed rgba(255,255,255,.55); cursor:move; }
body.ppz-pe .ppz-ov.ppz-sel { outline:2px solid #4dd2ff; touch-action:none; overflow:visible; }
body.ppz-pe [contenteditable] { outline:2px solid #ffd84d !important; background:rgba(255,216,77,.08); cursor:text; }
.ppz-rs { position:absolute; right:-4px; bottom:-4px; width:28px; height:28px; background:#4dd2ff; border-radius:8px;
  touch-action:none; cursor:nwse-resize; }
@media (pointer:coarse) { .ppz-rs { width:38px; height:38px; } }
body.ppz-pe #ppzGear, body.ppz-pe #ppzSet, body.ppz-pe #fab, body.ppz-pe #ppzPEbtn { display:none !important; }
#ppzPEui[hidden], #ppzPEbtn[hidden] { display:none !important; }
#ppzPEbar { position:fixed; top:0; left:0; right:0; z-index:70; display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:6px; align-items:center;
  padding:8px 10px; background:rgba(18,8,31,.96); border-bottom:1px solid var(--accent); }
#ppzPEbar b { margin-right:auto; font-size:.85rem; color:var(--accent2); }
#ppzPEui button { min-height:40px; min-width:42px; padding:0 10px; border-radius:10px; border:1px solid var(--line);
  background:var(--panel2); color:var(--text); font:inherit; font-size:.9rem; cursor:pointer; touch-action:manipulation; }
#ppzPEui button.pri { background:var(--accent); border-color:var(--accent); color:#12081f; font-weight:700; }
#ppzPEui button.bad { border-color:var(--bad); color:var(--bad); }
#ppzPEp { position:fixed; left:0; right:0; bottom:0; z-index:70; max-height:44vh; overflow:auto;
  padding:10px 12px calc(10px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom)); background:rgba(29,15,51,.97); border-top:1px solid var(--accent); }
#ppzPEp .r { display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:6px; align-items:flex-end; margin:6px 0; }
#ppzPEp label { display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:3px; font-size:.75rem; color:var(--muted); flex:1 1 130px; }
#ppzPEp label.ck { flex-direction:row; align-items:center; gap:8px; flex:0 0 auto; font-size:.85rem; color:var(--text); }
#ppzPEp input, #ppzPEp select { font:inherit; font-size:16px; padding:7px; border-radius:8px; border:1px solid var(--line);
  background:var(--bg); color:var(--text); }
#ppzPEp input[type=color] { height:40px; padding:3px; }
#ppzPEp input[type=range] { padding:0; accent-color:var(--accent); }
#ppzPEp input[type=checkbox] { width:22px; height:22px; accent-color:var(--accent); }
#ppzPEp .hint { font-size:.85rem; color:var(--muted); margin:4px 0; }
#ppzPEbtn { position:fixed; right:16px; bottom:76px; z-index:40; min-height:44px; padding:0 16px; border-radius:999px;
  border:1px solid var(--accent); background:var(--panel); color:var(--text); font:inherit; font-weight:600;
  box-shadow:0 4px 18px rgba(0,0,0,.5); cursor:pointer; }`;
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* ---------- Sections ("anchors") and keys ---------- */
  let ANCH = [];
  function tagAnchors() {
    let n = 0; ANCH = [];
    document.querySelectorAll("header, footer, .box").forEach(el => {
      if (el.closest("#dev, #modal, #ppzSet, #ppzPEui, [data-ppzv]")) return;
      if (el.parentElement && el.parentElement.closest(".box, header, footer")) return;
      const key = el.id ? "#" + el.id : el.tagName === "HEADER" ? "header" : el.tagName === "FOOTER" ? "footer" : "box" + (n++);
      el.dataset.ppzk = key;
      ANCH.push({ el, key, parent: el.parentNode });
    });
  }
  const real = el => [...el.children].filter(c => !c.hasAttribute("data-ppzv"));
  function keyOf(el) {
    const path = []; let cur = el;
    while (cur && !(cur.dataset && cur.dataset.ppzk)) {
      const p = cur.parentElement; if (!p) return null;
      const i = real(p).indexOf(cur); if (i < 0) return null;
      path.unshift(i); cur = p;
    }
    return cur ? cur.dataset.ppzk + (path.length ? ">" + path.join(".") : "") : null;
  }
  function byKey(k) {
    if (!k) return null;
    const [ak, rest] = k.split(">");
    const a = ANCH.find(x => x.key === ak); if (!a) return null;
    let el = a.el;
    if (rest) for (const i of rest.split(".").map(Number)) { el = real(el)[i]; if (!el) return null; }
    return el;
  }
  const domCmp = (x, y) => (x.el.compareDocumentPosition(y.el) & Node.DOCUMENT_POSITION_FOLLOWING) ? -1 : 1;
  function groups() {
    const m = new Map();
    ANCH.forEach(a => { if (!m.has(a.parent)) m.set(a.parent, []); m.get(a.parent).push(a); });
    return [...m.values()];
  }
  function placeGroup(list, sorter) {
    const parent = list[0].parent;
    const inDom = list.filter(a => a.el.parentNode === parent);
    if (inDom.length < 2) return;
    const first = inDom.slice().sort(domCmp)[0].el;
    const mark = document.createComment("ppz");
    parent.insertBefore(mark, first);
    sorter(inDom).forEach(a => parent.insertBefore(a.el, mark));
    mark.remove();
  }

  /* ---------- Apply saved layout to the page ---------- */
  let editing = false, selKey = null, selShape = null, drag = null;
  const styled = new Set(), ORIGTXT = new Map();
  function applyOrder() {
    const order = S().pageOrder;
    if (Array.isArray(order) && order.length) {
      groups().forEach(list => placeGroup(list, l => l.slice().sort((x, y) => {
        const a = order.indexOf(x.key), b = order.indexOf(y.key);
        return (a < 0 ? 1e3 + ANCH.indexOf(x) : a) - (b < 0 ? 1e3 + ANCH.indexOf(y) : b);
      })));
    }
    const fb = $("ppzFavBox"), tb = $("top5") && $("top5").closest(".box");
    if (fb && tb) tb.before(fb);
  }
  function applyText() {
    const tx = S().pageText || {};
    for (const k in tx) {
      const el = byKey(k);
      if (!el || el.children.length) continue;
      if (!ORIGTXT.has(k)) ORIGTXT.set(k, el.textContent);
      if (el.textContent !== tx[k]) el.textContent = tx[k];
    }
  }
  function applyLayout() {
    styled.forEach(el => { el.style.translate = ""; el.classList.remove("ppz-hid", "ppz-hidE"); });
    styled.clear();
    const L = lay();
    for (const k in L) {
      const el = byKey(k), v = L[k]; if (!el || !v) continue;
      if (v.x || v.y) el.style.translate = (v.x || 0) + "px " + (v.y || 0) + "px";
      if (v.hide) el.classList.add(editing ? "ppz-hidE" : "ppz-hid");
      styled.add(el);
    }
  }
  function renderOverlay() {
    document.querySelectorAll(".ppz-ov").forEach(e => e.remove());
    document.querySelectorAll(".ppz-anc").forEach(e => e.classList.remove("ppz-anc"));
    shapes().forEach(s => {
      const a = ANCH.find(x => x.key === s.a) || ANCH[0]; if (!a) return;
      a.el.classList.add("ppz-anc");
      const isBtn = s.type === "btn";
      const e = document.createElement(isBtn && !editing ? "a" : "div");
      e.className = "ppz-ov ppz-" + s.type + (editing && s.id === selShape ? " ppz-sel" : "");
      e.dataset.id = s.id; e.setAttribute("data-ppzv", "");
      Object.assign(e.style, {
        left: s.x + "%", top: s.y + "px", width: s.w + "%", height: s.h + "px",
        zIndex: s.back ? -1 : 5, opacity: s.op ?? 1,
        transform: s.rot ? "rotate(" + s.rot + "deg)" : "",
        borderRadius: s.type === "circle" ? "50%" : (s.r ?? (isBtn ? 10 : 8)) + "px"
      });
      if (s.type === "rect" || s.type === "circle" || isBtn) e.style.background = s.fill || "#a66bff";
      if (s.type === "img") {
        const src = img(s.img || "");
        if (src) e.style.backgroundImage = 'url("' + String(src).replace(/"/g, "%22") + '")';
        else if (editing) { e.textContent = "🖼 pick a picture"; e.style.background = "rgba(255,255,255,.08)"; }
        e.style.backgroundSize = s.fit === "fit" ? "contain" : "cover";
      }
      if (s.type === "text" || isBtn) {
        e.textContent = s.text || "";
        e.style.color = s.color || "#fff";
        e.style.fontSize = (s.size || 18) + "px";
        if (s.font) e.style.fontFamily = '"' + s.font + '", sans-serif';
        if (s.bold) e.style.fontWeight = "700";
      }
      if (isBtn && !editing) { e.href = safeHref(s.href); e.target = "_blank"; e.rel = "noopener"; }
      if (editing && s.id === selShape) { const h = document.createElement("span"); h.className = "ppz-rs"; e.appendChild(h); }
      a.el.appendChild(e);
    });
  }
  function markSel() {
    document.querySelectorAll(".ppz-selpage").forEach(e => e.classList.remove("ppz-selpage"));
    const el = editing && selKey ? byKey(selKey) : null;
    if (el) el.classList.add("ppz-selpage");
  }
  function applyAll() { applyOrder(); applyText(); applyLayout(); renderOverlay(); markSel(); }

  tagAnchors();
  const basePage = renderPage;
  renderPage = function () { basePage.apply(this, arguments); applyAll(); };
  applyAll();

  /* ---------- Keep exports clean ---------- */
  if (typeof buildHTML === "function") {
    const baseBuild = buildHTML;
    buildHTML = function () {
      if (editing) exit();
      groups().forEach(list => placeGroup(list, l => l.slice().sort((x, y) => ANCH.indexOf(x) - ANCH.indexOf(y))));
      styled.forEach(el => { el.style.translate = ""; el.classList.remove("ppz-hid", "ppz-hidE"); if (!el.getAttribute("style")) el.removeAttribute("style"); });
      document.querySelectorAll(".ppz-anc").forEach(e => e.classList.remove("ppz-anc"));
      ANCH.forEach(a => a.el.removeAttribute("data-ppzk"));
      try { return baseBuild.apply(this, arguments); }
      finally { ANCH.forEach(a => a.el.dataset.ppzk = a.key); applyAll(); }
    };
  }

  if (!DEV) return; /* everything below is for the owner only */

  /* ---------- Editor UI ---------- */
  const ui = document.createElement("div");
  ui.id = "ppzPEui"; ui.hidden = true; ui.setAttribute("data-ppzv", "");
  ui.innerHTML = `
    <div id="ppzPEbar"><b>✏️ Editing page</b>
      <button data-pe="add" data-t="rect" title="Rectangle">▭</button>
      <button data-pe="add" data-t="circle" title="Circle">◯</button>
      <button data-pe="add" data-t="text" title="Text">🔤</button>
      <button data-pe="add" data-t="img" title="Picture">🖼</button>
      <button data-pe="add" data-t="btn" title="Link button">🔗</button>
      <button data-pe="undo" title="Undo">↶</button>
      <button data-pe="done" class="pri">✅ Done</button></div>
    <div id="ppzPEp"></div>`;
  document.body.appendChild(ui);
  const peBtn = document.createElement("button");
  peBtn.id = "ppzPEbtn"; peBtn.type = "button"; peBtn.textContent = "✏️ Edit page"; peBtn.hidden = true;
  peBtn.setAttribute("data-ppzv", "");
  document.body.appendChild(peBtn);
  peBtn.onclick = () => enter();
  const fab = $("fab");
  const syncBtn = () => { peBtn.hidden = editing || !fab || fab.hidden || !isUnlocked(); };
  if (fab) new MutationObserver(syncBtn).observe(fab, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ["hidden"] });
  syncBtn();

  function enter() {
    if (!isUnlocked()) return toast("Log in first");
    if ($("dev").classList.contains("open")) closeDev();
    editing = true; selKey = selShape = null;
    document.body.classList.add("ppz-pe"); ui.hidden = false; syncBtn();
    refresh(); toast("Tap something to select it");
  }
  function exit() {
    finishText();
    editing = false; selKey = selShape = null;
    document.body.classList.remove("ppz-pe"); ui.hidden = true;
    applyAll(); syncBtn();
  }
  function refresh() { applyLayout(); renderOverlay(); markSel(); panel(); }
  const shape = () => shapes().find(s => s.id === selShape);
  let saveT = 0;
  const queueSave = () => { clearTimeout(saveT); saveT = setTimeout(save, 400); };

  /* ---------- Bottom panel ---------- */
  const arrows = () => ["←,-4,0", "↑,0,-4", "↓,0,4", "→,4,0"].map(a => {
    const [t, dx, dy] = a.split(",");
    return `<button data-pe="n" data-dx="${dx}" data-dy="${dy}">${t}</button>`;
  }).join("");
  function panel() {
    const p = $("ppzPEp"); if (!p || !editing) return;
    const s = shape();
    if (s) { p.innerHTML = shapePanel(s); return; }
    const el = selKey && byKey(selKey);
    if (!el) {
      p.innerHTML = `<p class="hint">👆 <b>Tap</b> anything to select it. <b>Tap it again and drag</b> to move it.<br>
        Use the buttons at the top to add shapes, text, pictures or link buttons.</p>`;
      return;
    }
    const a = el.dataset.ppzk ? ANCH.find(x => x.key === selKey) : null;
    const grp = a ? ANCH.filter(x => x.parent === a.parent) : [];
    const v = lay()[selKey] || {};
    const txt = norm(el.textContent);
    const canText = !el.children.length && txt;
    p.innerHTML = `
      <div class="r"><b style="font-size:.85rem">Selected: ${el.tagName.toLowerCase()}${txt ? ' · "' + esc(txt.slice(0, 30)) + (txt.length > 30 ? "…" : "") + '"' : ""}</b></div>
      <div class="r">${arrows()}<button data-pe="parent">⬆ Bigger part</button></div>
      <div class="r">
        ${canText ? '<button data-pe="text">✏️ Edit text</button>' : ""}
        <button data-pe="hide">${v.hide ? "👁 Show" : "🙈 Hide"}</button>
        <button data-pe="reset">↺ Reset</button>
        ${grp.length > 1 ? '<button data-pe="up">▲ Section up</button><button data-pe="down">▼ Section down</button>' : ""}
      </div>
      ${!canText && txt ? '<p class="hint">To change words, tap right on the words themselves.</p>' : ""}`;
  }
  function shapePanel(s) {
    const t = s.type, has = (...ts) => ts.includes(t);
    const pics = [["", "(pick a picture)"], ...Object.keys(D.images || {}).map(k => [k, k])];
    return `
      <div class="r"><b style="font-size:.85rem">${NAMES[t] || "Shape"}</b></div>
      <div class="r">
        ${has("text", "btn") ? `<label style="flex-basis:100%">Text<input data-sf="text" value="${esc(s.text || "")}"></label>` : ""}
        ${has("btn") ? `<label style="flex-basis:100%">Link<input data-sf="href" value="${esc(s.href || "")}" placeholder="https://..."></label>` : ""}
        ${has("img") ? `<label>Picture<select data-sf="img">${opts(pics, s.img || "")}</select></label>
          <label>Fit<select data-sf="fit">${opts([["fill", "Fill (may crop)"], ["fit", "Fit (whole picture)"]], s.fit || "fill")}</select></label>` : ""}
        ${has("rect", "circle", "btn") ? `<label>Fill<input type="color" data-sf="fill" value="${esc(s.fill || "#a66bff")}"></label>` : ""}
        ${has("text", "btn") ? `<label>Text color<input type="color" data-sf="color" value="${esc(s.color || "#ffffff")}"></label>
          <label>Font<select data-sf="font">${opts(FONTS, s.font || "")}</select></label>
          <label>Size ${s.size || 18}px<input type="range" data-sf="size" min="10" max="80" value="${s.size || 18}"></label>
          <label class="ck"><input type="checkbox" data-sf="bold" ${s.bold ? "checked" : ""}>Bold</label>` : ""}
        ${has("rect", "img", "btn") ? `<label>Corners<input type="range" data-sf="r" min="0" max="60" value="${s.r ?? (t === "btn" ? 10 : 8)}"></label>` : ""}
        <label>See-through<input type="range" data-sf="op" min="10" max="100" value="${Math.round((s.op ?? 1) * 100)}"></label>
        <label>Rotate<input type="range" data-sf="rot" min="-180" max="180" value="${s.rot || 0}"></label>
        <label class="ck"><input type="checkbox" data-sf="back" ${s.back ? "checked" : ""}>Behind text</label>
      </div>
      <div class="r">${arrows()}
        <button data-pe="dup">⧉ Copy</button>
        <button data-pe="del" class="bad">🗑 Delete</button></div>`;
  }
  const P = $("ppzPEp");
  const onField = e => {
    const f = e.target.dataset && e.target.dataset.sf; if (!f) return;
    const s = shape(); if (!s) return;
    let v = e.target.type === "checkbox" ? e.target.checked : e.target.value;
    if (["size", "r", "rot"].includes(f)) v = +v;
    if (f === "op") v = +v / 100;
    s[f] = v;
    renderOverlay(); queueSave();
    if (e.type === "change" && f === "img") panel();
  };
  P.addEventListener("input", onField);
  P.addEventListener("change", onField);

  /* ---------- Toolbar + panel buttons ---------- */
  ui.addEventListener("click", e => {
    const b = e.target.closest("[data-pe]"); if (!b) return;
    const act = b.dataset.pe;
    if (act === "done") return exit();
    if (act === "undo") { finishText(); if (ACT.undo) ACT.undo(); setTimeout(refresh, 0); return; }
    if (act === "add") return addShape(b.dataset.t);
    if (act === "n") return nudge(+b.dataset.dx, +b.dataset.dy);
    if (act === "parent") {
      const el = byKey(selKey); if (!el) return;
      if (el.dataset.ppzk) return toast("That's already the whole section");
      selKey = keyOf(el.parentElement); refresh(); return;
    }
    if (act === "text") return startText();
    if (act === "hide") {
      const L = layW(), v = (L[selKey] ||= {});
      v.hide = !v.hide; if (!v.hide) delete v.hide;
      if (!Object.keys(v).length) delete L[selKey];
      refresh(); save(); return;
    }
    if (act === "reset") {
      delete layW()[selKey];
      if (S().pageText && S().pageText[selKey] != null) {
        delete S().pageText[selKey];
        const o = ORIGTXT.get(selKey), el = byKey(selKey);
        if (o != null && el) el.textContent = o;
        ORIGTXT.delete(selKey);
      }
      refresh(); save(); toast("Reset"); return;
    }
    if (act === "up" || act === "down") return moveSection(act === "up" ? -1 : 1);
    if (act === "dup") {
      const s = shape(); if (!s) return;
      const c = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(s));
      c.id = newId(); c.x = r1(c.x + 3); c.y += 12;
      shapesW().push(c); selShape = c.id; refresh(); save(); return;
    }
    if (act === "del") {
      const list = shapesW(), i = list.findIndex(s => s.id === selShape);
      if (i >= 0) list.splice(i, 1);
      selShape = null; refresh(); save(); toast("Deleted"); return;
    }
  });

  /* ---------- Actions ---------- */
  const newId = () => "s" + Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 5);
  function anchorOf(s) { const a = ANCH.find(x => x.key === s.a) || ANCH[0]; return a ? a.el : null; }
  function nudge(dx, dy) {
    const s = shape();
    if (s) { const a = anchorOf(s); const w = a ? a.getBoundingClientRect().width || 1 : 1; s.x = r1(s.x + dx / w * 100); s.y += dy; }
    else if (selKey) { const v = (layW()[selKey] ||= {}); v.x = (v.x || 0) + dx; v.y = (v.y || 0) + dy; }
    else return;
    refresh(); queueSave();
  }
  function moveSection(dir) {
    const a = ANCH.find(x => x.key === selKey); if (!a) return;
    const grp = ANCH.filter(x => x.parent === a.parent);
    const order = grp.slice().sort(domCmp).map(x => x.key);
    const i = order.indexOf(a.key), j = i + dir;
    if (j < 0 || j >= order.length) return toast(dir < 0 ? "Already at the top" : "Already at the bottom");
    [order[i], order[j]] = [order[j], order[i]];
    const keys = grp.map(x => x.key);
    S().pageOrder = [...(S().pageOrder || []).filter(k => !keys.includes(k)), ...order];
    applyOrder(); refresh(); save();
    a.el.scrollIntoView({ block: "center", behavior: "smooth" });
  }
  function addShape(t) {
    finishText();
    const cy = innerHeight * 0.4;
    const a = ANCH.find(x => { const r = x.el.getBoundingClientRect(); return r.top <= cy && r.bottom >= cy; }) || ANCH[0];
    if (!a) return toast("No section found");
    const r = a.el.getBoundingClientRect();
    const accent = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue("--accent").trim() || "#a66bff";
    const s = { id: newId(), type: t, a: a.key, x: 35, y: Math.max(0, Math.round(cy - r.top - 40)), w: 30, h: 80, op: 1, rot: 0 };
    if (t === "rect") Object.assign(s, { fill: accent, r: 12, op: .85 });
    if (t === "circle") Object.assign(s, { fill: accent, w: 18, h: Math.round(r.width * .18), op: .85 });
    if (t === "text") Object.assign(s, { text: "New text", color: "#ffffff", size: 22, w: 40, h: 50 });
    if (t === "btn") Object.assign(s, { text: "Open", href: "https://", fill: accent, color: "#12081f", size: 16, w: 26, h: 46, bold: true });
    if (t === "img") Object.assign(s, { img: Object.keys(D.images || {})[0] || "", fit: "fill", h: 140 });
    shapesW().push(s); selShape = s.id; selKey = null;
    refresh(); save();
  }

  /* ---------- Editing text right on the page ---------- */
  let textEl = null, textKey = null, origText = "";
  function startText() {
    const el = byKey(selKey); if (!el) return;
    if (el.children.length) return toast("Tap right on the words you want to change");
    textEl = el; textKey = selKey; origText = norm(el.textContent);
    try { el.contentEditable = "plaintext-only"; } catch (e) { el.contentEditable = "true"; }
    el.focus();
    const rg = document.createRange(); rg.selectNodeContents(el);
    const sel = getSelection(); sel.removeAllRanges(); sel.addRange(rg);
    toast("Type, then tap somewhere else (or press Enter)");
  }
  function walk(o, path, hits, want) {
    if (typeof o === "string") { if (norm(o) === want) hits.push(path); return; }
    if (!o || typeof o !== "object") return;
    for (const k of Object.keys(o)) {
      if ((path.length === 0 || (path.length === 1 && path[0] === "site")) && SKIP.has(k)) continue;
      walk(o[k], path.concat(k), hits, want);
    }
  }
  function findPath(text, el) {
    const hits = []; walk(D, [], hits, text);
    if (!hits.length) return null;
    const t = el.closest(".tile[data-i]");
    if (t) { const h = hits.find(p => p[0] === "proxies" && String(p[1]) === t.dataset.i); if (h) return h; }
    return hits[0];
  }
  function setIn(path, v) {
    let o = D;
    for (let i = 0; i < path.length - 1; i++) o = o[path[i]];
    o[path[path.length - 1]] = v;
  }
  function finishText(cancel) {
    if (!textEl) return;
    const el = textEl, k = textKey; textEl = textKey = null;
    el.removeAttribute("contenteditable");
    if (cancel) { el.textContent = origText; return; }
    const nv = norm(el.textContent);
    if (!nv || nv === origText) { if (!nv) el.textContent = origText; return; }
    const path = findPath(origText, el);
    if (path) { setIn(path, nv); if (S().pageText) delete S().pageText[k]; }
    else { if (!ORIGTXT.has(k)) ORIGTXT.set(k, origText); (S().pageText ||= {})[k] = nv; }
    save(); toast(path ? "✅ Text updated" : "✅ Text updated on the page");
  }

  /* ---------- Tap, drag, resize ---------- */
  function pickEl(t) {
    if (t && t.nodeType !== 1) t = t.parentElement;
    while (t && t.hasAttribute("data-ppzv") && !t.classList.contains("ppz-ov")) t = t.parentElement;
    if (!t || t.closest("#dev, #modal, #ppzSet, #ppzGear, #fab, #ppzBg, #ppzPEui")) return null;
    return t.closest("[data-ppzk]") ? t : null;
  }
  document.addEventListener("pointerdown", e => {
    if (!editing || e.button > 0 || e.target.closest("#ppzPEui")) return;
    if (textEl && textEl.contains(e.target)) return;
    finishText();
    const node = e.target.closest(".ppz-ov");
    if (node) {
      const s = shapes().find(x => x.id === node.dataset.id); if (!s) return;
      if (selShape !== s.id) { selShape = s.id; selKey = null; refresh(); return; }
      e.preventDefault();
      const a = anchorOf(s);
      drag = { kind: "shape", s, id: e.pointerId, x0: e.clientX, y0: e.clientY, sx: s.x, sy: s.y, sw: s.w, sh: s.h,
        aw: a ? a.getBoundingClientRect().width || 1 : 1, rs: e.target.classList.contains("ppz-rs"), moved: false };
      try { node.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (err) {}
      return;
    }
    const el = pickEl(e.target); if (!el) return;
    const k = keyOf(el); if (!k) return;
    if (selKey !== k) { selKey = k; selShape = null; refresh(); return; }
    e.preventDefault();
    const v = lay()[k] || {};
    drag = { kind: "el", k, el, id: e.pointerId, x0: e.clientX, y0: e.clientY, sx: v.x || 0, sy: v.y || 0, moved: false };
    try { el.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (err) {}
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener("pointermove", e => {
    if (!drag || e.pointerId !== drag.id) return;
    const dx = e.clientX - drag.x0, dy = e.clientY - drag.y0;
    if (!drag.moved && Math.hypot(dx, dy) < 5) return;
    drag.moved = true;
    if (drag.kind === "el") {
      drag.nx = Math.round(drag.sx + dx); drag.ny = Math.round(drag.sy + dy);
      drag.el.style.translate = drag.nx + "px " + drag.ny + "px";
    } else {
      const s = drag.s, dxp = dx / drag.aw * 100;
      if (drag.rs) { s.w = r1(clamp(drag.sw + dxp, 2, 200)); s.h = Math.round(clamp(drag.sh + dy, 10, 3000)); }
      else { s.x = r1(drag.sx + dxp); s.y = Math.round(drag.sy + dy); }
      const n = document.querySelector('.ppz-ov[data-id="' + s.id + '"]');
      if (n) Object.assign(n.style, { left: s.x + "%", top: s.y + "px", width: s.w + "%", height: s.h + "px" });
    }
  });
  const endDrag = e => {
    if (!drag || e.pointerId !== drag.id) return;
    const d = drag; drag = null;
    if (!d.moved) return;
    if (d.kind === "el") { const L = layW(); L[d.k] = Object.assign({}, L[d.k], { x: d.nx, y: d.ny }); }
    save();
  };
  document.addEventListener("pointerup", endDrag);
  document.addEventListener("pointercancel", endDrag);

  /* Block normal clicks (links, cards, popups) while editing */
  document.addEventListener("click", e => {
    if (!editing || e.target.closest("#ppzPEui")) return;
    e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
  }, true);

  /* Keyboard: arrows nudge, Delete removes a shape, Esc deselects */
  document.addEventListener("keydown", e => {
    if (!editing) return;
    if (textEl) {
      if (e.key === "Enter" && !e.shiftKey) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); finishText(); }
      else if (e.key === "Escape") { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); finishText(true); }
      return;
    }
    if (e.target.closest && e.target.closest("input, textarea, select")) return;
    const step = e.shiftKey ? 10 : 2;
    const m = { ArrowLeft: [-step, 0], ArrowRight: [step, 0], ArrowUp: [0, -step], ArrowDown: [0, step] }[e.key];
    if (m && (selShape || selKey)) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); nudge(m[0], m[1]); return; }
    if ((e.key === "Delete" || e.key === "Backspace") && selShape) {
      e.preventDefault(); ui.querySelector('[data-pe="del"]') && ui.querySelector('[data-pe="del"]').click(); return;
    }
    if (e.key === "Escape") { selKey = selShape = null; refresh(); }
  }, true);

  /* ---------- Shortcut button inside the developer panel ---------- */
  const baseDev = renderDev;
  renderDev = function () {
    baseDev.apply(this, arguments);
    const body = $("devBody");
    if (!body || body.querySelector("#ppzPEopen")) return;
    const b = document.createElement("button");
    b.id = "ppzPEopen"; b.type = "button"; b.className = "btn full";
    b.style.cssText = "margin-bottom:12px;min-height:46px;font-weight:700";
    b.textContent = "✏️ Edit the page directly (move, hide, add shapes)";
    b.onclick = enter;
    body.prepend(b);
  };
  if ($("dev").classList.contains("open")) renderDev();
})();
</script>
<script id="ppz-addon-extras">
/* ===== ProxyPathz add-on: visitor cosmetics + guest canvas, link and preview fixes ===== */
(() => {
  if (window.__ppzExtras) return; window.__ppzExtras = true;
  const CKEY = "proxypathz_v15_cosmetics";
  const HEX = /^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i;
  const DEF = { cap: "", c1: "#a66bff", c2: "#2b6bff", accent: "", font: "", shape: "", hover: "", size: "", title: "", trail: "", burst: false };
  let C = Object.assign({}, DEF);
  try { C = Object.assign(C, JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(CKEY)) || {}); } catch (e) {}
  const saveC = () => { try { localStorage.setItem(CKEY, JSON.stringify(C)); } catch (e) {} };
  const motionOff = () => document.body.classList.contains("noanim") || document.body.classList.contains("vnoanim") ||
    matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;

  const CAPS = [["", "Site default"], ["default", "Default"], ["glass", "Glass"], ["solid", "Solid color"], ["gradient", "Gradient"],
    ["animated", "Animated"], ["neon", "Neon glow"], ["shine", "Shine"], ["none", "None"]];
  const ACCENTS = [["", "Site default"], ["#a66bff", "Purple"], ["#4d8dff", "Blue"], ["#ff5cc8", "Pink"], ["#3ddc84", "Green"],
    ["#ff9a3d", "Orange"], ["#ff5c7a", "Red"], ["#ffd84d", "Gold"], ["custom", "Custom…"]];
  const FONTS = [["", "Site default"], ["Poppins", "Poppins"], ["Orbitron", "Orbitron"], ["Russo One", "Russo One"],
    ["Space Grotesk", "Space Grotesk"], ["JetBrains Mono", "JetBrains Mono"]];
  const SHAPES = [["", "Default"], ["round", "Rounded"], ["sharp", "Sharp"], ["pill", "Extra round"]];
  const HOVERS = [["", "Default"], ["lift", "Lift"], ["glow", "Glow"], ["tilt", "Tilt"], ["none", "None"]];
  const SIZES = [["", "Normal"], ["compact", "Compact"], ["big", "Big"]];
  const TITLES = [["", "Default"], ["glow", "Glow"], ["rainbow", "Rainbow"], ["neon", "Neon flicker"], ["plain", "Plain"]];
  const TRAILS = [["", "Off"], ["sparkle", "Sparkles"], ["stars", "Stars"], ["bubble", "Bubbles"]];
  const ok = (list, v) => list.some(x => x[0] === v) ? v : "";
  const o = (list, cur) => list.map(([v, l]) => `<option value="${v}" ${v === cur ? "selected" : ""}>${l}</option>`).join("");

  /* ---------- Styles ---------- */
  const css = document.createElement("style");
  css.id = "ppzExtrasCss"; css.setAttribute("data-ppzv", "");
  css.textContent = `
html[data-vshape="round"] .tile { border-radius:22px !important; }
html[data-vshape="sharp"] .tile { border-radius:4px !important; }
html[data-vshape="pill"] .tile { border-radius:34px !important; }
html[data-vhover] .tile { transition:transform .2s ease, box-shadow .2s ease; }
html[data-vhover="lift"] .tile:hover { transform:translateY(-6px); }
html[data-vhover="glow"] .tile:hover { box-shadow:0 0 0 2px var(--accent),0 0 26px var(--accent); }
html[data-vhover="tilt"] .tile:hover { transform:rotate(-1.5deg) scale(1.03); }
html[data-vhover="none"] .tile:hover { transform:none !important; box-shadow:none !important; }
html[data-vsize="compact"] .grid { grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(120px,1fr)) !important; }
html[data-vsize="big"] .grid { grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(250px,1fr)) !important; }
html[data-vtitle="glow"] #siteTitle { text-shadow:0 0 10px var(--accent),0 0 24px var(--accent) !important; }
html[data-vtitle="rainbow"] #siteTitle { background:linear-gradient(90deg,#ff5c7a,#ffd84d,#3ddc84,#4dd2ff,#a66bff,#ff5c7a) 0 0/200% 100% !important;
  -webkit-background-clip:text !important; background-clip:text !important; color:transparent !important;
  -webkit-text-fill-color:transparent !important; animation:ppzRain 4s linear infinite !important; }
@keyframes ppzRain { to { background-position:200% 0; } }
html[data-vtitle="neon"] #siteTitle { background:none !important; color:#fff !important; -webkit-text-fill-color:#fff !important;
  text-shadow:0 0 4px #fff,0 0 12px var(--accent),0 0 28px var(--accent) !important; animation:ppzFlick 3s infinite !important; }
@keyframes ppzFlick { 0%,19%,21%,23%,55%,57%,100% { opacity:1; } 20%,22%,56% { opacity:.55; } }
html[data-vtitle="plain"] #siteTitle { background:none !important; color:var(--text) !important;
  -webkit-text-fill-color:currentColor !important; text-shadow:none !important; animation:none !important; }
#ppzFx { position:fixed; inset:0; pointer-events:none; z-index:95; overflow:hidden; }
.ppzP { position:absolute; font-size:13px; line-height:1; color:var(--accent); text-shadow:0 0 6px var(--accent);
  animation:ppzPart .8s ease-out forwards; will-change:transform,opacity; }
.ppzP-bubble { width:10px; height:10px; border:1.5px solid var(--accent); border-radius:50%; }
.ppzP-burst { width:7px; height:7px; border-radius:50%; background:var(--accent); box-shadow:0 0 8px var(--accent); }
@keyframes ppzPart { from { transform:translate(-50%,-50%) scale(1); opacity:1; }
  to { transform:translate(calc(-50% + var(--dx)),calc(-50% + var(--dy))) scale(.3); opacity:0; } }
#ppzCosSec input[type=color] { width:44px; height:36px; padding:2px; border-radius:8px; border:1px solid var(--line); background:var(--panel2); }
#gv { height:auto !important; aspect-ratio:4/3; width:100%; max-width:720px; margin-left:auto; margin-right:auto; }
.preview-note { font-size:.8rem; color:var(--muted); margin:6px 0; }
.preview-note a { color:var(--accent2); font-weight:600; }`;
  document.head.appendChild(css);
  const dyn = document.createElement("style");
  dyn.id = "ppzExtrasDyn"; dyn.setAttribute("data-ppzv", "");
  document.head.appendChild(dyn);

  /* ---------- Apply cosmetics ---------- */
  const H = document.documentElement;
  function setAttr(name, v) { v ? H.setAttribute(name, v) : H.removeAttribute(name); }
  function apply() {
    setAttr("data-vshape", ok(SHAPES, C.shape));
    setAttr("data-vhover", ok(HOVERS, C.hover));
    setAttr("data-vsize", ok(SIZES, C.size));
    setAttr("data-vtitle", ok(TITLES, C.title));
    let t = "";
    if (HEX.test(C.accent)) t += `html { --accent:${C.accent} !important; }`;
    const f = ok(FONTS, C.font);
    if (f) t += `body, body *:not(.ppz-ov):not(.ci):not(code):not(pre) { font-family:"${f}", sans-serif !important; }`;
    dyn.textContent = t;
  }
  apply();

  /* Visitor's own card title bar */
  if (typeof tile === "function") {
    const baseTile = tile;
    tile = function () {
      const h = baseTile.apply(this, arguments);
      const s = ok(CAPS, C.cap); if (!s) return h;
      const c1 = HEX.test(C.c1) ? C.c1 : "#a66bff", c2 = HEX.test(C.c2) ? C.c2 : "#2b6bff";
      const rep = `<div class="info cs-${s} $1" style="--c1:${c1};--c2:${c2};">`;
      const out = h.replace(/<div class="info cs-[\w-]+ (ct-[\w-]+)" style="[^"]*">/, rep);
      return out !== h ? out : h.replace('<div class="info">', rep.replace("$1", "ct-auto"));
    };
  }

  /* ---------- Mouse trail + tap burst ---------- */
  const fx = document.createElement("div");
  fx.id = "ppzFx"; fx.setAttribute("data-ppzv", ""); fx.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
  document.body.appendChild(fx);
  function spawn(x, y, kind, n) {
    if (motionOff() || document.body.classList.contains("ppz-pe") || fx.childElementCount > 70) return;
    for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
      const p = document.createElement("span");
      p.className = "ppzP ppzP-" + kind;
      if (kind === "sparkle") p.textContent = "✦";
      if (kind === "stars") p.textContent = "★";
      let dx, dy;
      if (kind === "burst") { const a = (Math.PI * 2 * k) / n; dx = Math.cos(a) * 42; dy = Math.sin(a) * 42; }
      else { dx = (Math.random() - .5) * 26; dy = 14 + Math.random() * 18; }
      p.style.left = x + "px"; p.style.top = y + "px";
      p.style.setProperty("--dx", dx.toFixed(1) + "px"); p.style.setProperty("--dy", dy.toFixed(1) + "px");
      fx.appendChild(p);
      setTimeout(() => p.remove(), 850);
    }
  }
  let lastTrail = 0;
  document.addEventListener("pointermove", e => {
    if (!C.trail || e.pointerType !== "mouse") return;
    const now = performance.now(); if (now - lastTrail < 45) return; lastTrail = now;
    spawn(e.clientX, e.clientY, ok(TRAILS, C.trail) || "sparkle", 1);
  }, { passive: true });
  document.addEventListener("pointerdown", e => { if (C.burst) spawn(e.clientX, e.clientY, "burst", 8); }, { passive: true });

  /* ---------- ⚙ Settings section ---------- */
  const panel = $("ppzSet");
  function addSection() {
    if (!panel || !panel.lastElementChild || panel.querySelector("#ppzCosSec")) return;
    const accSel = C.accent ? (ACCENTS.some(a => a[0] === C.accent) ? C.accent : "custom") : "";
    const sec = document.createElement("div");
    sec.id = "ppzCosSec";
    sec.innerHTML = `<h3 style="margin-top:12px">✨ Cosmetics</h3>
      <label><span>Card title bar<small>Your own style</small></span><select data-c="cap">${o(CAPS, ok(CAPS, C.cap))}</select></label>
      <label><span>Title bar colors<small>For Solid, Gradient, Animated, Neon</small></span>
        <span style="display:flex;gap:6px"><input type="color" data-c="c1" value="${HEX.test(C.c1) ? C.c1 : "#a66bff"}">
        <input type="color" data-c="c2" value="${HEX.test(C.c2) ? C.c2 : "#2b6bff"}"></span></label>
      <label><span>Theme color</span><select data-c="accentSel">${o(ACCENTS, accSel)}</select></label>
      ${accSel === "custom" ? `<label><span>Custom color</span><input type="color" data-c="accent" value="${HEX.test(C.accent) ? C.accent : "#a66bff"}"></label>` : ""}
      <label><span>Font</span><select data-c="font">${o(FONTS, ok(FONTS, C.font))}</select></label>
      <label><span>Card corners</span><select data-c="shape">${o(SHAPES, ok(SHAPES, C.shape))}</select></label>
      <label><span>Card hover</span><select data-c="hover">${o(HOVERS, ok(HOVERS, C.hover))}</select></label>
      <label><span>Card size</span><select data-c="size">${o(SIZES, ok(SIZES, C.size))}</select></label>
      <label><span>Title effect</span><select data-c="title">${o(TITLES, ok(TITLES, C.title))}</select></label>
      <label><span>Mouse trail<small>Mouse or trackpad only</small></span><select data-c="trail">${o(TRAILS, ok(TRAILS, C.trail))}</select></label>
      <label><span>Tap burst<small>Little burst where you tap</small></span><input type="checkbox" data-c="burst" ${C.burst ? "checked" : ""}></label>
      <div class="row"><button type="button" id="ppzCosReset">Reset cosmetics</button></div>`;
    panel.insertBefore(sec, panel.lastElementChild);
    const redraw = () => { sec.remove(); addSection(); };
    const onChange = e => {
      const k = e.target.dataset.c; if (!k) return;
      const v = e.target.type === "checkbox" ? e.target.checked : e.target.value;
      if (k === "accentSel") {
        C.accent = v === "custom" ? (HEX.test(C.accent) ? C.accent : "#a66bff") : v;
        saveC(); apply(); if (e.type === "change") redraw(); return;
      }
      C[k] = v; saveC(); apply();
      if (["cap", "c1", "c2"].includes(k)) renderPage();
    };
    sec.addEventListener("change", onChange);
    sec.addEventListener("input", e => { if (e.target.type === "color") onChange(e); });
    sec.querySelector("#ppzCosReset").onclick = () => {
      if (!confirm("Reset all cosmetics to the site default?")) return;
      C = Object.assign({}, DEF); saveC(); apply(); renderPage(); redraw();
    };
  }
  if (panel) { new MutationObserver(addSection).observe(panel, { childList: true }); addSection(); }

  /* ---------- Fix: guest canvas keeps the same shape on every screen ---------- */
  const gv = $("gv");
  if (gv && typeof paintGuest === "function") {
    const setScale = () => gv.style.setProperty("--gs", ((gv.clientWidth || 720) / 720).toFixed(4));
    new ResizeObserver(setScale).observe(gv); setScale();
    const basePaint = paintGuest;
    paintGuest = function () {
      basePaint.apply(this, arguments);
      gv.querySelectorAll(".ci").forEach(el => {
        const it = G.find(g => g.id === el.dataset.id);
        if (it) el.style.fontSize = "calc(" + it.size + "px * var(--gs, 1))";
      });
    };
    paintGuest();
  }

  /* ---------- Fix: hide empty links like "https://" ---------- */
  if (typeof openProxy === "function") {
    const baseOpen = openProxy;
    openProxy = function () {
      baseOpen.apply(this, arguments);
      const sheet = $("sheet"); if (!sheet) return;
      sheet.querySelectorAll("a.link").forEach(a => {
        let good = false;
        try { const u = new URL(a.getAttribute("href")); good = /^https?:$/.test(u.protocol) && u.hostname.includes("."); } catch (e) {}
        if (!good) a.remove();
      });
    };
  }

  /* ---------- Fix: explain blank previews ---------- */
  if (ACT && ACT.previewSite) {
    const basePrev = ACT.previewSite;
    ACT.previewSite = function (el) {
      basePrev.call(this, el);
      const box = $("previewBox"), fr = box && box.querySelector("iframe");
      if (!fr || box.querySelector(".preview-note")) return;
      const note = document.createElement("p");
      note.className = "preview-note";
      note.innerHTML = 'Blank or "refused to connect"? That site doesn\'t allow previews. ' +
        '<a href="' + esc(fr.src) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open it normally ↗</a>';
      fr.after(note);
    };
  }

  /* ---------- Keep visitor cosmetics out of exported files ---------- */
  if (typeof buildHTML === "function") {
    const baseBuild = buildHTML;
    const ATTRS = ["data-vshape", "data-vhover", "data-vsize", "data-vtitle"];
    buildHTML = function () {
      const saved = ATTRS.map(a => [a, H.getAttribute(a)]);
      ATTRS.forEach(a => H.removeAttribute(a));
      try { return baseBuild.apply(this, arguments); }
      finally { saved.forEach(([a, v]) => v && H.setAttribute(a, v)); }
    };
  }

  renderPage();
})();
</script>
<script id="ppz-addon-organize">
/* ===== ProxyPathz add-on: tabbed settings, panel color themes, categories ===== */

#ppzSet [hidden] { display:none !important; }
#ppzTabs { display:flex; gap:6px; overflow-x:auto; padding:2px 0 10px; margin-bottom:6px; border-bottom:1px solid var(--line); scrollbar-width:none; }

#ppzTabs button { flex:none; min-height:36px; padding:0 12px; border-radius:999px; border:1px solid var(--line);
  background:var(--panel2); color:var(--text); font:inherit; font-size:.8rem; cursor:pointer; white-space:nowrap; touch-action:manipulation; }
#ppzTabs button.on { background:var(--accent); border-color:var(--accent); color:#12081f; font-weight:700; }
#ppzSet .ppz-tabbed > h3:first-child { display:none; }
.ppzThemes { display:grid; grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(84px,1fr)); gap:8px; margin:8px 0; }
.ppzThemes button { min-height:64px; border-radius:12px; border:2px solid var(--line); cursor:pointer; font:inherit; font-size:.75rem;
  font-weight:600; display:flex; align-items:flex-end; justify-content:center; padding:6px; touch-action:manipulation; }
.ppzThemes button.on { border-color:var(--accent); box-shadow:0 0 0 2px var(--accent); }
html[data-vtheme] body { background-color:var(--bg) !important; }

html[data-vtheme="light"] #ppzGear { box-shadow:0 4px 14px rgba(0,0,0,.15); }
#ppzChips { display:flex; gap:8px; overflow-x:auto; padding:10px 0 4px; scrollbar-width:none; }
#ppzChips::-webkit-scrollbar { display:none; }

  background:var(--panel2); color:var(--text); font:inherit; font-size:.85rem; cursor:pointer; white-space:nowrap; touch-action:manipulation; }
#ppzChips button.on, .ppzTagPick button.on { background:var(--accent); border-color:var(--accent); color:#12081f; font-weight:700; }
.ppzTagPick { display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:6px; margin:-4px 0 12px; }
.ppzTagPick button { min-height:34px; padding:0 10px; font-size:.8rem; }`;
  document.head.appendChild(css);
  const dyn = document.createElement("style");
  dyn.id = "ppzOrgDyn"; dyn.setAttribute("data-ppzv", "");
  document.head.appendChild(dyn);

  /* ---------- Panel color themes ---------- */
  function themeVars() {
    if (TH.theme === "custom" && HEX.test(TH.custom)) {
      const n = parseInt(TH.custom.slice(1), 16), r = n >> 16, g = (n >> 8) & 255, b = n & 255;
      const light = (0.2126 * r + 0.7152 * g + 0.0722 * b) / 255 > 0.55, X = TH.custom;
      return light
        ? { mode: "light", bg: `color-mix(in srgb, ${X} 88%, #000)`, panel: X, panel2: `color-mix(in srgb, ${X} 93%, #000)`,
            line: `color-mix(in srgb, ${X} 78%, #000)`, text: "#1a1424", muted: "#5d5470" }
        : { mode: "dark", bg: `color-mix(in srgb, ${X} 60%, #000)`, panel: X, panel2: `color-mix(in srgb, ${X} 88%, #fff)`,
            line: `color-mix(in srgb, ${X} 70%, #fff)`, text: "#f1eef7", muted: "#a9a1bb" };
    }
    const t = THEMES[TH.theme];
    return t && t.bg ? t : null;
  }
  function applyTheme() {
    const v = themeVars();
    if (!v) { H.removeAttribute("data-vtheme"); dyn.textContent = ""; return; }
    H.setAttribute("data-vtheme", v.mode);
    dyn.textContent = `html { --bg:${v.bg} !important; --panel:${v.panel} !important; --panel2:${v.panel2} !important;
      --line:${v.line} !important; --text:${v.text} !important; --muted:${v.muted} !important; color-scheme:${v.mode}; }`;
  }
  applyTheme();

  /* ---------- Settings: Colors section ---------- */
  const panel = $("ppzSet");
  function addThemeSection() {
    if (!panel || !panel.lastElementChild || panel.querySelector("#ppzThemeSec")) return;
    const sec = document.createElement("div");
    sec.id = "ppzThemeSec";
    const sw = Object.entries(THEMES).map(([k, t]) => {
      const bg = k === "" ? "linear-gradient(135deg,#12081f,#1d0f33)" : k === "custom"
        ? "conic-gradient(#ff5c7a,#ffd84d,#3ddc84,#4dd2ff,#a66bff,#ff5c7a)" : `linear-gradient(135deg,${t.bg},${t.panel})`;
      const fg = t.mode === "light" ? "#1d1430" : "#fff";
      return `<button type="button" data-theme="${k}" class="${(TH.theme || "") === k ? "on" : ""}"
        style="background:${bg};color:${fg};text-shadow:${t.mode === "light" ? "none" : "0 1px 3px #000"}">${t.name}</button>`;
    }).join("");
    sec.innerHTML = `<h3 style="margin-top:12px">🎨 Panel colors</h3>
      <p class="note" style="margin-top:0">Changes the boxes, menus and background. Saved on this device.</p>
      <div class="ppzThemes">${sw}</div>
      ${TH.theme === "custom" ? `<label><span>Your panel color<small>Text color adjusts automatically</small></span>
        <input type="color" id="ppzThemeCustom" value="${HEX.test(TH.custom) ? TH.custom : "#1d0f33"}"
        style="width:54px;height:38px;padding:2px;border-radius:8px;border:1px solid var(--line);background:var(--panel2)"></label>` : ""}`;
    panel.insertBefore(sec, panel.lastElementChild);
    sec.querySelector(".ppzThemes").addEventListener("click", e => {
      const b = e.target.closest("[data-theme]"); if (!b) return;
      TH.theme = b.dataset.theme; saveTH(); applyTheme();

    const ci = sec.querySelector("#ppzThemeCustom");
    if (ci) ci.addEventListener("input", () => { TH.custom = ci.value; saveTH(); applyTheme(); });
  }

  /* ---------- Settings: tabs ---------- */
  const TABS = [["general", "⚙ General"], ["colors", "🎨 Colors"], ["bar", "🏷 Title bar"], ["bg", "🌌 Background"], ["cos", "✨ Cosmetics"]];
  const SEC = { colors: "#ppzThemeSec", bar: "#ppzBarSec", bg: "#ppzVBgSec", cos: "#ppzCosSec" };
  let active = "general", tabKey = "", orgT = 0;
  function showTab() {
    const gen = panel.querySelector("#ppzTabGen");
    if (gen) gen.hidden = active !== "general";
    for (const k in SEC) { const s = panel.querySelector(SEC[k]); if (s) { s.hidden = active !== k; s.classList.add("ppz-tabbed"); } }
    panel.querySelectorAll("#ppzTabs button").forEach(b => b.classList.toggle("on", b.dataset.tab === active));
  }
  function organize() {
    if (!panel || !panel.firstElementChild) return;
    const title = panel.firstElementChild, last = panel.lastElementChild;
    if (title === last) return;
    let bar = panel.querySelector("#ppzTabs");
    if (!bar) { bar = document.createElement("div"); bar.id = "ppzTabs"; title.after(bar); tabKey = ""; }
    let gen = panel.querySelector("#ppzTabGen");
    if (!gen) { gen = document.createElement("div"); gen.id = "ppzTabGen"; bar.after(gen); }
    [...panel.children].forEach(ch => {
      if (ch === title || ch === bar || ch === gen || ch === last) return;
      if (ch.matches(Object.values(SEC).join(","))) return;
      gen.appendChild(ch);
    });
    const avail = TABS.filter(([k]) => k === "general" || panel.querySelector(SEC[k]));
    if (!avail.some(([k]) => k === active)) active = "general";
    const key = avail.map(a => a[0]).join(",");
    if (key !== tabKey) {
      tabKey = key;
      bar.innerHTML = avail.map(([k, l]) => `<button type="button" data-tab="${k}">${l}</button>`).join("");
    }
    showTab();
  }
  if (panel) {
    new MutationObserver(() => { addThemeSection(); clearTimeout(orgT); orgT = setTimeout(organize, 0); })
      .observe(panel, { childList: true });
    panel.addEventListener("click", e => {
      const b = e.target.closest("#ppzTabs [data-tab]");
      if (b) { active = b.dataset.tab; showTab(); panel.scrollTop = 0; }
      e.stopPropagation(); /* stops the panel from closing when a button inside it redraws */
    });
    addThemeSection(); organize();
  }

  /* ---------- Categories on the page ---------- */
  const QUICK = [["Games", "🎮"], ["Video", "🎬"], ["Browsing", "🌐"], ["Music", "🎵"], ["Social", "💬"], ["Other", "📦"]];
  const EMO = Object.fromEntries(QUICK.map(([t, e]) => [t.toLowerCase(), e]));
  let cat = "all", chipKey = "";
  const favs = () => { try { return (JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("proxypathz_v15_visitor")) || {}).favs || []; } catch (e) { return []; } };
  const search = $("search"), all = $("all");
  let chips = null, emptyMsg = null;
  if (search && all) {
    chips = document.createElement("div");
    chips.id = "ppzChips"; chips.setAttribute("data-ppzv", "");
    search.after(chips);
    emptyMsg = document.createElement("div");
    emptyMsg.className = "empty"; emptyMsg.hidden = true; emptyMsg.setAttribute("data-ppzv", "");
    emptyMsg.textContent = "No proxies in this category.";
    all.after(emptyMsg);
    chips.addEventListener("click", e => {
      const b = e.target.closest("[data-cat]"); if (!b) return;
      cat = b.dataset.cat; drawChips(true); filter();
    });
    new MutationObserver(() => { drawChips(); filter(); }).observe(all, { childList: true });
    drawChips(); filter();
  }
  function drawChips(force) {
    const tags = new Map();
    D.proxies.forEach(p => (p.tags || []).forEach(t => {
      const k = String(t).trim().toLowerCase(); if (k && !tags.has(k)) tags.set(k, String(t).trim());
    }));
    const hasFav = favs().length > 0;
    const list = [["all", "All"], ...(hasFav ? [["__fav", "⭐ Favorites"]] : []), ["__work", "✅ Working"],
      ...[...tags].map(([k, label]) => [k, (EMO[k] ? EMO[k] + " " : "") + label])];
    if (!list.some(x => x[0] === cat)) cat = "all";
    const key = list.map(x => x.join(":")).join("|") + "#" + cat;
    if (key === chipKey && !force) return;
    chipKey = key;
    chips.innerHTML = list.map(([k, l]) => `<button type="button" data-cat="${esc(k)}" class="${k === cat ? "on" : ""}">${esc(l)}</button>`).join("");
  }
  function filter() {
    if (!all) return;
    const f = favs();
    let shown = 0;
    all.querySelectorAll(".tile[data-i]").forEach(t => {
      const p = D.proxies[+t.dataset.i];
      const okCat = !p ? true
        : cat === "all" ? true
        : cat === "__fav" ? f.includes(p.name)
        : cat === "__work" ? (p.links || []).some(l => l.status === "working")
        : (p.tags || []).some(x => String(x).trim().toLowerCase() === cat);
      t.style.display = okCat ? "" : "none";
      if (okCat) shown++;
    });
    const hasTiles = !!all.querySelector(".tile");
    emptyMsg.hidden = !(hasTiles && shown === 0);
  }
  document.addEventListener("click", e => { if (e.target.closest(".ppz-star")) setTimeout(() => { drawChips(); filter(); }, 0); });

  /* ---------- Quick category buttons in the Proxies tab ---------- */
  if (typeof PUBLIC_BUILD === "undefined" || !PUBLIC_BUILD) {
    function drawPick(box, i, inp) {
      const p = D.proxies[i]; if (!p) return;
      const has = new Set((p.tags || []).map(t => String(t).trim().toLowerCase()));
      box.innerHTML = QUICK.map(([t, e]) => `<button type="button" data-tag="${t}" class="${has.has(t.toLowerCase()) ? "on" : ""}">${e} ${t}</button>`).join("");
      box.onclick = ev => {
        const b = ev.target.closest("[data-tag]"); if (!b) return;
        const t = b.dataset.tag, tags = (p.tags || []).slice();
        const k = tags.findIndex(x => String(x).trim().toLowerCase() === t.toLowerCase());
        k >= 0 ? tags.splice(k, 1) : tags.push(t);
        p.tags = tags; inp.value = tags.join(", ");
        save(); drawPick(box, i, inp);
      };
    }
    function addPickers() {
      if (typeof tab === "undefined" || tab !== "proxies") return;
      document.querySelectorAll('#devBody input[data-path$=".tags"]').forEach(inp => {
        const lab = inp.closest("label");
        if (!lab || (lab.nextElementSibling && lab.nextElementSibling.classList.contains("ppzTagPick"))) return;
        const i = +inp.dataset.path.split(".")[1];
        const box = document.createElement("div"); box.className = "ppzTagPick";
        lab.after(box); drawPick(box, i, inp);
        inp.addEventListener("input", () => setTimeout(() => drawPick(box, i, inp), 0));
      });
    }
    const baseDev = renderDev;
    renderDev = function () { baseDev.apply(this, arguments); addPickers(); };
    if ($("dev").classList.contains("open")) renderDev();
  }

  /* ---------- Keep visitor theme out of exported files ---------- */
  if (typeof buildHTML === "function") {
    const baseBuild = buildHTML;
    buildHTML = function () {
      const v = H.getAttribute("data-vtheme"); H.removeAttribute("data-vtheme");
      const hidden = [...document.querySelectorAll("#all .tile")].filter(t => t.style.display === "none");
      hidden.forEach(t => t.style.display = "");
      try { return baseBuild.apply(this, arguments); }
      finally { if (v) H.setAttribute("data-vtheme", v); hidden.forEach(t => t.style.display = "none"); }
    };
  }
})();
</script>
<script id="ppz-addon-style2">
/* ===== ProxyPathz add-on: new title bars + drawing editor, title effects, hover fix, top category bar ===== */
(() => {
  if (window.__ppzStyle2) return; window.__ppzStyle2 = true;
  const VKEY = "proxypathz_v15_titlebar", DKEY = "proxypathz_v15_capdraw", AKEY = "proxypathz_v15_catarrange";
  const HEX = /^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i;
  const load = (k, d) => { try { return Object.assign(d, JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(k)) || {}); } catch (e) { return d; } };
  let V = load(VKEY, { cap: "", c1: "#a66bff", c2: "#2b6bff", te: "" });
  const saveV = () => { try { localStorage.setItem(VKEY, JSON.stringify(V)); } catch (e) {} };
  const getDraw = () => localStorage.getItem(DKEY) || "";
  const unlocked = () => typeof isUnlocked === "function" && (typeof PUBLIC_BUILD === "undefined" || !PUBLIC_BUILD) && isUnlocked();

  /* Migrate old cosmetics title settings once */
  try {
    const old = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("proxypathz_v15_cosmetics")) || {};
    if (!V.cap && old.cap) { V.cap = old.cap; if (HEX.test(old.c1)) V.c1 = old.c1; if (HEX.test(old.c2)) V.c2 = old.c2; }
    if (!V.te && old.title) V.te = old.title;
    saveV();
  } catch (e) {}

  const BARS = [
    ["", "Site default"], ["default", "Default"], ["frosted", "Frosted"], ["holo", "Holo"], ["midnight", "Midnight"],
    ["candy", "Candy"], ["sunset", "Sunset"], ["aqua", "Aqua"], ["toxic", "Toxic"], ["gold", "Gold"], ["carbon", "Carbon"],
    ["pixel", "Pixel"], ["outline", "Outline"], ["neon", "Neon"], ["duo", "Your colors"], ["duoflow", "Your colors (moving)"],
    ["solid", "Solid"], ["none", "Text only"], ["drawn", "🎨 Custom drawing"]];
  const OLD = { glass: "frosted", gradient: "duo", animated: "duoflow", shine: "holo" };
  const DARK = new Set(["holo", "candy", "gold"]);
  const USES_COLORS = new Set(["duo", "duoflow", "solid", "neon"]);
  const TES = [["", "Default"], ["glow", "Glow"], ["rainbow", "Rainbow"], ["neon", "Neon"], ["fire", "Fire"],
    ["ice", "Ice"], ["glitch", "Glitch"], ["outline", "Outline"], ["plain", "Plain"]];
  const lum = h => { const n = parseInt(h.slice(1), 16); return (0.2126 * (n >> 16) + 0.7152 * ((n >> 8) & 255) + 0.0722 * (n & 255)) / 255; };
